const positions = finished.positions; const normals = finished.normals; const count = positions.length / 3; // hand2 的顶点色里已经乘进了缝隙遮蔽和横褶的压暗，这两样每一帧都不一样，先原样除掉，留下不随姿态变的底色 const colors = new Float32Array(finished.colors); for (let v = 0; v < count; v++) { const o = v * 3; const x = positions[o]; const y = positions[o + 1]; const z = positions[o + 2]; const blocked = 0.4 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, normals[o], normals[o + 1], normals[o + 2], 0.002) + 0.3 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, normals[o], normals[o + 1], normals[o + 2], 0.005) + 0.2 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, normals[o], normals[o + 1], normals[o + 2], 0.01) + 0.1 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, normals[o], normals[o + 1], normals[o + 2], 0.018); let shade = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.pow(Math.max(0, 1 - blocked), 1.1); if (model.creases.length) shade *= 1 - 0.18 * Math.min(1, hand2CreaseDepth(model, x, y, z) / 0.0004); colors[o] /= shade; colors[o + 1] /= shade; colors[o + 2] /= shade; } const skin = hand4BindWeights(model, positions, finished.indices); // 袖口里面和腕带以下的部分不受任何动作影响，每帧照抄第一帧 const fixed = new Uint8Array(count); for (let v = 0; v < count; v++) fixed[v] = positions[v * 3 + 1] < -0.015 ? 1 : 0; return { count, positions, normals, colors, indices: finished.indices, bones: skin.bones, weights: skin.weights, masks: skin.masks, fixed, restFrames: hand4BoneFrames(model), }; } // ---------- 预先计算一帧 ---------- // 贴回曲面：从 scratch 里的点出发，沿 mask 选中的那些部位的距离场梯度走（四点法求梯度，四个采样的平均值当作这一点的距离）， // 直到落在曲面上、走满 10 步、或者走过的路程超过 budget。 // scratch 的 0 到 2 是位置，3 到 5 是最后一次求出的法线，6 是走过的路程。返回是否落在了曲面上。 function hand4Project(model, mask, scratch, budget) { const e = 0.0007; const tolerance = hand4Settings.settle; let x = scratch[0]; let y = scratch[1]; let z = scratch[2]; let nx = 0; let ny = 0; let nz = 1; let path = 0; let settled = false; for (let pass = 0; pass < 10; pass++) { const f1 = hand4Field(model, x + e, y - e, z - e, mask); const f2 = hand4Field(model, x - e, y - e, z + e, mask); const f3 = hand4Field(model, x - e, y + e, z - e, mask); const f4 = hand4Field(model, x + e, y + e, z + e, mask); const gx = f1 - f2 - f3 + f4; const gy = -f1 - f2 + f3 + f4; const gz = -f1 + f2 - f3 + f4; const length = Math.sqrt(gx * gx + gy * gy + gz * gz) || 1e-9; nx = gx / length; ny = gy / length; nz = gz / length; const d = 0.25 * (f1 + f2 + f3 + f4); if (d < tolerance && d > -tolerance) { settled = true; break; } if (pass === 9 || path > budget) break; // 牛顿步：距离除以梯度的模。融合处梯度的模小于 1，步子相应放大；限幅防止跳过头 let step = (d * 4 * e) / length; step = step > 0.004 ? 0.004 : step < -0.004 ? -0.004 : step; x -= nx * step; y -= ny * step; z -= nz * step; path += step < 0 ? -step : step; } scratch[0] = x; scratch[1] = y; scratch[2] = z; scratch[3] = nx; scratch[4] = ny; scratch[5] = nz; scratch[6] = path; return settled; } // 输出：顶点位置；把静止姿态的坐标轴带到这一帧的转动（存 X、Y 两条轴，法线和贴图方向都由它得到）； // 这一帧的明暗（缝隙遮蔽乘横褶压暗，0 到 255）。 function hand4BakeFrame(rest, coordinate, first) { const model = hand4BuildModel(hand4PoseAt(coordinate)); const skin = hand4SkinTransforms(rest.restFrames, hand4BoneFrames(model)); const count = rest.count; const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3); const axes = new Int8Array(count * 6); const shade = new Uint8Array(count); const M = skin.matrices; const Q = skin.quats; const P0 = rest.positions; const N0 = rest.normals; const W = rest.weights; const B = rest.bones; const wide = 0.0016; const settings = hand4Settings; const hasCreases = model.creases.length > 0; const scratch = new Float64Array(7); let unsettled = 0; let buried = 0; for (let v = 0; v < count; v++) { const o = v * 3; if (first && rest.fixed[v]) { positions[o] = first.positions[o]; positions[o + 1] = first.positions[o + 1]; positions[o + 2] = first.positions[o + 2]; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) axes[v * 6 + i] = first.axes[v * 6 + i]; shade[v] = first.shade[v]; continue; } // 一、线性蒙皮。同时把各根骨头的转动（四元数）按同样的权重混合，后面用来转贴图方向 const px = P0[o]; const py = P0[o + 1]; const pz = P0[o + 2]; let x = 0; let y = 0; let z = 0; let qx = 0; let qy = 0; let qz = 0; let qw = 0; for (let slot = 0; slot < 4; slot++) { const w = W[v * 4 + slot]; if (w === 0) continue; const m = B[v * 4 + slot] * 12; x += w * (M[m] * px + M[m + 1] * py + M[m + 2] * pz + M[m + 3]); y += w * (M[m + 4] * px + M[m + 5] * py + M[m + 6] * pz + M[m + 7]); z += w * (M[m + 8] * px + M[m + 9] * py + M[m + 10] * pz + M[m + 11]); const q = B[v * 4 + slot] * 4; const signed = qx * Q[q] + qy * Q[q + 1] + qz * Q[q + 2] + qw * Q[q + 3] < 0 ? -w : w; qx += signed * Q[q]; qy += signed * Q[q + 1]; qz += signed * Q[q + 2]; qw += signed * Q[q + 3]; } // 二、贴回这一帧的曲面。 // 先直接在整只手的距离场上找落点。挪一小段就到了，或者落点本来就在这个顶点自己所属部位的表面上 // （关节外侧被蒙皮拉塌的地方），就用它。 // 否则说明这个顶点被别的部位盖住了（指尖按进掌心、手指互相挤住、拇指压在手指上）。 // 这时不能让它顺着梯度一路滑到接缝上去，那样接触的一瞬间一大片顶点会在表面上滑走。 // 做法是分两步：先在自己部位的曲面上找落点，再从那里往整只手的曲面上挪，后一步限制距离—— // 贴着接缝的一小圈顶点挪到融合出来的圆角上，埋得深的留在自己部位的表面上（在里面，看不见）。 scratch[0] = x; scratch[1] = y; scratch[2] = z; let settled = hand4Project(model, 63, scratch, settings.otherFar); let hidden = false; let direct = settled && scratch[6] <= settings.otherNear; if (!direct && settled) direct = Math.abs(hand4Field(model, scratch[0], scratch[1], scratch[2], rest.masks[v])) < 0.0003; let nx = scratch[3]; let ny = scratch[4]; let nz = scratch[5]; if (direct) { x = scratch[0]; y = scratch[1]; z = scratch[2]; } else { // 直接找到的落点和路程先留着：顶点本来就在自己部位的表面上时，第二步和它是同一回事，不用重算 let targetX = scratch[0]; let targetY = scratch[1]; let targetZ = scratch[2]; let otherPath = scratch[6]; scratch[0] = x; scratch[1] = y; scratch[2] = z; hand4Project(model, rest.masks[v], scratch, settings.ownFar); const ownPath = scratch[6]; const ownShare = hand2Smooth(settings.ownFar, settings.ownNear, ownPath); const ownNx = scratch[3]; const ownNy = scratch[4]; const ownNz = scratch[5]; if (ownShare > 0 && ownPath > 0.0001) { x += (scratch[0] - x) * ownShare; y += (scratch[1] - y) * ownShare; z += (scratch[2] - z) * ownShare; scratch[0] = x; scratch[1] = y; scratch[2] = z; settled = hand4Project(model, 63, scratch, settings.otherFar); targetX = scratch[0]; targetY = scratch[1]; targetZ = scratch[2]; nx = scratch[3]; ny = scratch[4]; nz = scratch[5]; otherPath = scratch[6]; } const otherShare = ownShare > 0 ? hand2Smooth(settings.otherFar, settings.otherNear, otherPath) : 0; x += (targetX - x) * otherShare; y += (targetY - y) * otherShare; z += (targetZ - z) * otherShare; if (otherShare < 1) { nx = ownNx + (nx - ownNx) * otherShare; ny = ownNy + (ny - ownNy) * otherShare; nz = ownNz + (nz - ownNz) * otherShare; const length = Math.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny + nz * nz) || 1; nx /= length; ny /= length; nz /= length; settled = true; } hidden = otherShare <= 0; } if (hidden) buried++; else if (!settled) unsettled++; positions[o] = x; positions[o + 1] = y; positions[o + 2] = z; // 被埋住的顶点看不见，不用再算法线的修正和遮蔽 let light = 0.5; if (!hidden) { // 三、折缝里梯度会在两侧来回跳。凹进去的地方再用大一倍多的步长求一次梯度，两者差得多就改用后者 let near = hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, 0.002); if (near > 0.25) { const f1 = hand4Field(model, x + wide, y - wide, z - wide, 63); const f2 = hand4Field(model, x - wide, y - wide, z + wide, 63); const f3 = hand4Field(model, x - wide, y + wide, z - wide, 63); const f4 = hand4Field(model, x + wide, y + wide, z + wide, 63); const gx = f1 - f2 - f3 + f4; const gy = -f1 - f2 + f3 + f4; const gz = -f1 + f2 - f3 + f4; const length = Math.sqrt(gx * gx + gy * gy + gz * gz) || 1e-9; const agree = (gx * nx + gy * ny + gz * nz) / length; if (agree < 0.85) { const keep = agree < 0.4 ? 0 : (agree - 0.4) / 0.45; const bx = gx / length + (nx - gx / length) * keep; const by = gy / length + (ny - gy / length) * keep; const bz = gz / length + (nz - gz / length) * keep; const blended = Math.sqrt(bx * bx + by * by + bz * bz) || 1; nx = bx / blended; ny = by / blended; nz = bz / blended; near = hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, 0.002); } } // 四、缝隙遮蔽和横褶压暗（公式和 hand2 相同） const blocked = 0.4 * near + 0.3 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, 0.005) + 0.2 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, 0.01) + 0.1 * hand4Blocked(model, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, 0.018); light = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.pow(Math.max(0, 1 - blocked), 1.1); if (hasCreases) light *= 1 - 0.18 * Math.min(1, hand4CreaseDepth(model, x, y, z) / 0.0004); } shade[v] = Math.round(255 * light); // 五、这个顶点处的转动：混合后的骨头转动，再补一个最小的转动，把蒙皮得到的法线对准距离场的法线 const quatLength = Math.sqrt(qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz + qw * qw) || 1; qx /= quatLength; qy /= quatLength; qz /= quatLength; qw /= quatLength; let ax = 1 - 2 * (qy * qy + qz * qz); let ay = 2 * (qx * qy + qz * qw); let az = 2 * (qx * qz - qy * qw); let bx = 2 * (qx * qy - qz * qw); let by = 1 - 2 * (qx * qx + qz * qz); let bz = 2 * (qy * qz + qx * qw); const cx = ay * bz - az * by; const cy = az * bx - ax * bz; const cz = ax * by - ay * bx; const mx = ax * N0[o] + bx * N0[o + 1] + cx * N0[o + 2]; const my = ay * N0[o] + by * N0[o + 1] + cy * N0[o + 2]; const mz = az * N0[o] + bz * N0[o + 1] + cz * N0[o + 2]; const cos = mx * nx + my * ny + mz * nz; if (cos > -0.95) { const rx = my * nz - mz * ny; const ry = mz * nx - mx * nz; const rz = mx * ny - my * nx; const k = 1 / (1 + cos); const da = (rx * ax + ry * ay + rz * az) * k; const db = (rx * bx + ry * by + rz * bz) * k; const tax = ax * cos + (ry * az - rz * ay) + rx * da; const tay = ay * cos + (rz * ax - rx * az) + ry * da; const taz = az * cos + (rx * ay - ry * ax) + rz * da; const tbx = bx * cos + (ry * bz - rz * by) + rx * db; const tby = by * cos + (rz * bx - rx * bz) + ry * db; const tbz = bz * cos + (rx * by - ry * bx) + rz * db; ax = tax; ay = tay; az = taz; bx = tbx; by = tby; bz = tbz; } axes[v * 6] = Math.round(ax * 127); axes[v * 6 + 1] = Math.round(ay * 127); axes[v * 6 + 2] = Math.round(az * 127); axes[v * 6 + 3] = Math.round(bx * 127); axes[v * 6 + 4] = Math.round(by * 127); axes[v * 6 + 5] = Math.round(bz * 127); } return { positions, axes, shade, unsettled, buried }; } // 后台线程的脚本：各帧之间互不依赖，可以分给几个线程同时算。 // src/js/ 里的文件共用一个函数作用域，线程里拿不到，所以把用得到的函数和数据按名字抄一份过去。 function hand4WorkerSource() { const parts = { hand2Add, hand2Sub, hand2Mul, hand2Mad, hand2Lerp, hand2Dot, hand2Cross, hand2Length, hand2Unit, hand2Orthogonal, hand2Smooth, hand2FingerDefs, hand2Metacarpals, hand2WristDepth, hand2ThumbDef, hand2ThumbTipShape, hand2Poses, hand2FingerChain, hand2ThumbChain, hand2Stride, hand2GroupStride, hand2PushCone, hand2PushBall, hand2PushFlatCone, hand2CloseGroup, hand2PushPhalanx, hand2PrimDistance, hand2StrapTab, hand4Settings, hand4Open, hand4ThumbVia, hand4FistDrop, hand4ThumbLiftAmount, hand4Timing, hand4QuatFromAxes, hand4QuatSlerp, hand4QuatAxisY, hand4QuatAxisZ, hand4Mix, hand4ThumbFromFrames, hand4ThumbQuats, hand4ThumbFromDirections, hand4ThumbFromQuats, hand4ThumbBlend, hand4ThumbArc, hand4ThumbLift, hand4Keys, hand4PoseAt, hand4BuildModel, hand4CreaseDepth, hand4Field, hand4Blocked, hand4Project, hand4BoneFrames, hand4SkinTransforms, hand4BakeFrame, }; ; // ---- 压力脊上的碎冰 ---- function buildFrostRubble(G, heightAt) { let THREE = G.THREE, random = il(7741), geometries = [], matrix = new THREE.Matrix4(), rotation = new THREE.Quaternion(), euler = new THREE.Euler(), position = new THREE.Vector3(), scale = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1), iceColor = [0.34, 0.56, 0.72], snowColor = [0.76, 0.8, 0.85]; for (let ridge of FROST_PRESSURE_RIDGES) { let points = ridge.points; for (let i = 0; i < points.length - 1; i++) { let ax = points[i][0], az = points[i][1], bx = points[i + 1][0], bz = points[i + 1][1], length = Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az); for (let walked = 0; walked < length; walked += 0.9 + random() * 1.1) { let t = walked / length, x = ax + (bx - ax) * t + (random() - 0.5) * ridge.halfWidth * 0.9, z = az + (bz - az) * t + (random() - 0.5) * ridge.halfWidth * 0.9, at = frostRidgeAt(ridge, x, z); // 只在冰垄隆起的地段堆冰块；离得太远的看不见，不放 if (at.core < 0.2 || frostPlayDistance(x, z) > 300) continue; let size = 0.7 + random() * random() * 2.9, geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size * (0.22 + random() * 0.4), size * (0.5 + random() * 0.5)); euler.set((random() - 0.5) * 1.5, random() * Math.PI * 2, (random() - 0.5) * 1.5); rotation.setFromEuler(euler); position.set(x, Math.max(0, heightAt(x, z)) + size * (0.02 + random() * 0.18), z); matrix.compose(position, rotation, scale); geometry.applyMatrix4(matrix); // 朝上的面落了雪，侧面是蓝色的冰 let normals = geometry.attributes.normal, colors = new Float32Array(normals.count * 3), tint = 0.8 + random() * 0.4; for (let k = 0; k < normals.count; k++) { let up = frostSmooth(0.25, 0.75, normals.getY(k)); for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) colors[k * 3 + c] = (iceColor[c] + (snowColor[c] - iceColor[c]) * up) * tint; } geometry.setAttribute(
; mesh.castShadow = false; mesh.receiveShadow = false; mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; return { mesh, triangles: geometries.length * 12 }; } // ---- 远处的人造物 ---- // 全部合并成三个网格：带顶点色的实体、贴在地上的影子、桅杆顶的红色障碍灯。 function buildFrostOutposts(G, heightAt, wind) { let THREE = G.THREE, random = il(90211), solids = [], lamps = [], shadowPositions = [], shadowUvs = [], shadowIndices = [], matrix = new THREE.Matrix4(), rotation = new THREE.Quaternion(), euler = new THREE.Euler(), position = new THREE.Vector3(), unit = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1), groundAt = (x, z) => Math.max(0, heightAt(x, z)), sunFlat = Math.hypot(Mn.dir.x, Mn.dir.z), shadeX = -Mn.dir.x / sunFlat, // 影子伸出去的方向 shadeZ = -Mn.dir.z / sunFlat, shadeStretch = sunFlat / Mn.dir.y, // 影子的长度是物体高度的多少倍 windYaw = Math.atan2(-wind.z, wind.x), // 让物体的 +x 轴指向下风方向的偏航角 color = { orange: [0.72, 0.17, 0.035], red: [0.56, 0.05, 0.035], white: [0.74, 0.75, 0.73], black: [0.03, 0.03, 0.035], steel: [0.36, 0.38, 0.4], dark: [0.1, 0.105, 0.115], blue: [0.05, 0.15, 0.4], olive: [0.16, 0.19, 0.09], wood: [0.3, 0.2, 0.11], yellow: [0.74, 0.5, 0.05], glass: [0.02, 0.03, 0.04], }; function addSolid(geometry, tint, x, y, z, yaw = 0, pitch = 0, roll = 0, target = solids) { euler.set(pitch, yaw, roll,

// 贴地的影子：从 (x, z) 沿背向太阳的方向伸出去，分段贴着地形起伏 function shadow(x, z, width, height, strength = 1) { let length = Math.min(85, height * shadeStretch), segments = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(length / 5)), sideX = -shadeZ * width * 0.5, sideZ = shadeX * width * 0.5, base = shadowPositions.length / 3; if (along < 130) shadow(x, z, 1.3 * size, 2.3 * size, 0.85); } } for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) drum(startX - dirX * 6 - dirZ * (k - 2) * 5, startZ - dirZ * 6 + dirX * (k - 2) * 5, color.red); } function insert(xz, y) { return [xz[0], y, xz[1]]; } let solidMesh = new THREE.Mesh(Ii(solids), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.72, metalness: 0.05 })); solidMesh.name =
; solidMesh.castShadow = false; solidMesh.receiveShadow = false; solidMesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; // 影子：把底下的颜色乘暗、偏蓝（阴影里只剩天光）。边缘和末端是虚的。 let shadowGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); shadowGeometry.setAttribute(
; lampMesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; lampMesh.castShadow = lampMesh.receiveShadow = false; let root = new THREE.Group(), clock = 0, triangles = 0; root.add(solidMesh, shadowMesh, lampMesh); for (let mesh of [solidMesh, shadowMesh, lampMesh]) { let geometry = mesh.geometry; triangles += (geometry.index ? geometry.index.count : geometry.attributes.position.count) / 3; } return { root, triangles, update(dt) { // 障碍灯：每两秒闪一下 clock += dt; let on = clock % 2 < 0.35 ? 1 : 0.06; lampMaterial.color.setRGB(14 * on, 0.5 * on, 0.25 * on); }, }; } // 主脊上最高的几座峰顶：沿主脊每隔一段，在垂直方向上找最高点，再留下比两边都高的。雪烟从这些点拖出去。 function frostCrestAnchors(heightAt, massif, count) { let stations = []; for (let along = -0.8; along <= 0.8001; along += 0.05) { let best = null; for (let across = -0.5; across <= 0.5001; across += 0.04) { let x = massif.x + along * massif.halfLength * massif.cos - across * massif.halfWidth * massif.sin, z = massif.z + along * massif.halfLength * massif.sin + across * massif.halfWidth * massif.cos, y = heightAt(x, z); if (!best || y > best.y) best = { x, y, z }; } stations.push(best); } return stations .filter((station, i) => i > 0 && i < stations.length - 1 && station.y >= stations[i - 1].y && station.y >= stations[i + 1].y && station.y > 50) .sort((a, b) => b.y - a.y) .slice(0, count); } async function createFrostBackdrop(G) { let THREE = G.THREE, root = new THREE.Group(), wind = frostWind(G), terrain = buildFrostTerrain(G, wind), heightAt = (x, z) => frostTerrainHeight(x, z, wind), rubble = buildFrostRubble(G, heightAt), outposts = buildFrostOutposts(G, heightAt, wind), weather = createFrostWeather(G, wind), plumes = createFrostPlumes(G, wind, frostCrestAnchors(heightAt, FROST_MASSIFS[0], 7), weather.uniforms.uTime); root.name =

+ ((error && error.stack) || error)); } }, stats: { tris: Math.round(terrain.triangles + rubble.triangles + outposts.triangles + weather.triangles + plumes.triangles), drawCalls: 6 + weather.drawCalls, }, }; } // 霜脊站的地面模块：风吹压实的雪面、蓝冰、踩出来的小路、雪地车的履带印、建筑脚下的脏雪、停机坪，以及贴地的吹雪。 // // 做法和港口的地面（08-ground.js）相同：一张大面片，着色器按世界坐标算出一切，不依赖贴图分辨率。 // - 两张数据贴图（画布现画，覆盖可玩范围）：哪里被踩实、哪里露冰、哪里脏、哪里有背风的雪堆和迎风的吹蚀、哪里被车压过。 // 其中

表现（太阳只有 12 度高，一点坡度明暗差别就很大），面片本身是平的，高度始终是 0。 // // 接口和港口的地面模块（08-ground.js 的 O1）一致：其他模块会调用 update、isPuddle、wetnessAt。新地图没有积水。 // 碰撞不在这里：物理模块把地面高度以下当作实心，不需要碰撞盒。 var FROST_GROUND = { // 风吹去的方向（世界 xz，单位向量）：从西北偏西吹向东南偏东。雪垄、吹雪带、积雪的迎风霜都用它。 wind: { x: Math.cos(Math.PI / 6), z: Math.sin(Math.PI / 6) }, // 数据贴图和俯视高度图覆盖的正方形范围（世界坐标） rect: { x0: -46, z0: -41, size: 82 }, // 停机坪：中心、半边长、顶面高度。和 40-frost-level.js 里那块 12 × 12 米、高 0.1 米的板一致 pad: { x: 18, z: 23, half: 6, top: 0.1 }, // 履带印折线段的数量上限（着色器里的数组长度） segments: 32, // 雪地车的履带印。每条是一串控制点 [x, z]，steps 是拆成几段直线，strength 是新旧程度（1 最新最深） tracks: [ [21.25, 12.9, 1.3, 0.9], [11.25, -14.7, 1.2, 0.8], [-14.6, 7.7, 0.8, 0.7], [-4.3, 8.5, 0.8, 0.7], [-10, 15.8, 0.8, 0.7], [-10, 17.2, 0.8, 0.7], [-24, 12.8, 0.8, 0.7], [-20, 13.2, 0.8, 0.7], ], // 露出来的蓝冰 [x, z, 沿风的半径, 横风的半径] ice: [ [-22, -5.5, 7, 3.3], [4.5, -18.5, 5.8, 2.8], [-2.5, 27, 6.2, 2.6], [-33, 24.5, 5, 2.9], [21, -30, 7.5, 2.5], [29, 9, 3.8, 1.9], ], // 车辆停放和掉头的地方：压实、有油渍 [x, z, 半径] parking: [ [4.6, -2, 3.4], [-35.8, -1.8, 2.6], [1.2, 17.2, 2.8], [-6.5, -17.6, 2.3], [10.8, 21.5, 2.2], ], }; // 固定种子的随机数，纹理每次生成都一样 function frostRandom(seed) { return () => { seed = (seed + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0; let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed); t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t; return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; } // 一条平滑曲线上等距取点。points 是 [x, z] 的数组，返回 steps + 1 个点。 function frostSmoothLine(THREE, points, steps) { let curve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3( points.map(([x, z]) => new THREE.Vector3(x, 0, z)), false,
, ); return curve.getSpacedPoints(steps).map((p) => [p.x, p.z]); } // 一个可平铺的高度场的坡度，编码成 0.5 - 坡度 × k 存进 8 位贴图；k 取成让 3 倍均方根刚好不溢出。 // unitX / unitY 是相邻两个像素之间的距离（任意单位，坡度就是
）。 function frostSlopes(field, size, unitX, unitY) { let count = size * size, sx = new Float32Array(count), sy = new Float32Array(count), sum = 0, wrap = (v) => (v + size) % size; for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) { let i = y * size + x, dx = (field[y * size + wrap(x + 1)] - field[y * size + wrap(x - 1)]) / (2 * unitX), dy = (field[wrap(y + 1) * size + x] - field[wrap(y - 1) * size + x]) / (2 * unitY); sx[i] = dx; sy[i] = dy; sum += dx * dx + dy * dy; } let rms = Math.sqrt(sum / (2 * count)) || 1, k = 0.16 / rms, encode = (v) => Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 - v * k)) * 255); return { sx, sy, k, encode }; } // 三张可平铺的噪声贴图。噪声函数用港口地面模块的 fs（分形噪声，最后一个参数是周期）。 // detail R 细颗粒 G 平滑的大团块 B 另一组平滑的大团块（分区用） A 中等大小的碎块 // slopeA RG 是 G 的坡度，BA 是 A 的坡度 // slopeB RG 是 R 的坡度 // scale 是三组坡度各自的解码系数（1 / k）。 function bakeFrostDetailMaps(size) { let count = size * size, grain = new Float32Array(count), smooth = new Float32Array(count), zone = new Float32Array(count), breakup = new Float32Array(count), stretch = (v) => Math.min(1, Math.max(0, 0.5 + (v - 0.5) * 2.2)); for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) { let u = x / size, v = y / size, i = y * size + x; grain[i] = stretch(fs(u * 96, v * 96, 3, 2, 96) * 0.6 + fs(u * 40, v * 40, 9, 2, 40) * 0.4); smooth[i] = stretch(fs(u * 4, v * 4, 21, 4, 4)); zone[i] = stretch(fs(u * 5, v * 5, 43, 4, 5)); breakup[i] = stretch(fs(u * 16, v * 16, 51, 3, 16)); } let detail = new Uint8Array(count * 4), slopeA = new Uint8Array(count * 4), slopeB = new Uint8Array(count * 4), unit = 1 / size, smoothSlope = frostSlopes(smooth, size, unit, unit), breakupSlope = frostSlopes(breakup, size, unit, unit), grainSlope = frostSlopes(grain, size, unit, unit); for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { detail[i * 4] = Math.round(grain[i] * 255); detail[i * 4 + 1] = Math.round(smooth[i] * 255); detail[i * 4 + 2] = Math.round(zone[i] * 255); detail[i * 4 + 3] = Math.round(breakup[i] * 255); slopeA[i * 4] = smoothSlope.encode(smoothSlope.sx[i]); slopeA[i * 4 + 1] = smoothSlope.encode(smoothSlope.sy[i]); slopeA[i * 4 + 2] = breakupSlope.encode(breakupSlope.sx[i]); slopeA[i * 4 + 3] = breakupSlope.encode(breakupSlope.sy[i]); slopeB[i * 4] = grainSlope.encode(grainSlope.sx[i]); slopeB[i * 4 + 1] = grainSlope.encode(grainSlope.sy[i]); slopeB[i * 4 + 2] = 128; slopeB[i * 4 + 3] = 128; } return { detail, slopeA, slopeB, scale: [1 / smoothSlope.k, 1 / breakupSlope.k, 1 / grainSlope.k] }; } // 雪垄（sastrugi）的高度图：风把雪面刻成一条条顺风的垄，迎风的一头陡而尖，背风的一头拖着长尾巴。 // 这里撒一批这种形状的
，取最高值。贴图的 u 沿风、v 横风，一张贴图对应 along × across 米，可平铺。 // R 高度（0..1） G、B 沿风和横风方向的坡度（每米，按高度为 1 算） function bakeFrostSastrugi(size, along, across) { let count = size * size, height = new Float32Array(count), random = frostRandom(20241), wrap = (v) => ((v % size) + size) % size, ease = (t) => (t <= 0 ? 0 : t >= 1 ? 1 : t * t * (3 - 2 * t)); for (let k = 0; k < 200; k++) { let centerU = random() * size, centerV = random() * size, length = ((1.1 + 2.7 * random() ** 2) / along) * size, halfWidth = ((0.16 + 0.3 * random()) / across) * size, amplitude = 0.35 + 0.65 * random(), skew = (random() - 0.5) * 0.44 * (along / across); for (let u = Math.floor(centerU - 0.1 * length) - 1; u <= Math.ceil(centerU + length) + 1; u++) { let t = (u - centerU) / length; if (t < -0.08 || t > 1) continue; let profile = t < 0.1 ? ease((t + 0.08) / 0.18) : Math.pow(1 - (t - 0.1) / 0.9, 1.3), width = halfWidth * (0.35 + 0.65 * ease((t + 0.08) / 0.3)) * (1 - 0.45 * Math.max(0, t)), center = centerV + skew * (u - centerU); for (let v = Math.floor(center - width); v <= Math.ceil(center + width); v++) { let c = Math.abs(v - center) / width; if (c >= 1) continue; let h = amplitude * profile * Math.pow(1 - c, 1.25), index = wrap(v) * size + wrap(u); if (h > height[index]) height[index] = h; } } } // 垄之间不是纯平的：垫一层低缓的起伏，再轻轻模糊一遍去掉像素台阶 let blurred = new Float32Array(count); for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) { let sum = 0; for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) sum += height[wrap(y + dy) * size + wrap(x + dx)] * (dx === 0 && dy === 0 ? 4 : dx === 0 || dy === 0 ? 2 : 1); blurred[y * size + x] = (sum / 16) * 0.86 + 0.14 * fs((x / size) * 8, (y / size) * 8, 61, 3, 8); } let slope = frostSlopes(blurred, size, along / size, across / size), data = new Uint8Array(count * 4); for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { data[i * 4] = Math.round(Math.min(1, blurred[i]) * 255); data[i * 4 + 1] = slope.encode(slope.sx[i]); data[i * 4 + 2] = slope.encode(slope.sy[i]); data[i * 4 + 3] = 255; } return { data, scale: 1 / slope.k }; } // 两张数据贴图，用画布现画。画布的 x 对应世界 x，y 对应世界 z，画的时候直接用世界坐标（米）。 // maskA R 人踩实的程度 G 蓝冰 B 脏（建筑和设备脚下、架空物体的下面） // maskB R 背风的雪堆 G 迎风一侧被吹蚀的硬壳 B 车辆压实 // 三个通道用加色混合分别画，互不干扰。边界都是软的，着色器里再用噪声打散。 function drawFrostGroundMasks(G, size) { let THREE = G.THREE, { rect, wind } = FROST_GROUND, scale = size / rect.size, make = () => { let canvas = document.createElement(
; // 软边的线：同一条线画四遍，一遍比一遍细，叠起来中间实、两边虚 function softLine(ctx, points, width, channel, strength) { ctx.strokeStyle =
; for (let k of [1, 0.72, 0.46, 0.24]) { ctx.lineWidth = width * k; ctx.beginPath(); points.forEach(([x, z], i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(x, z) : ctx.moveTo(x, z))); ctx.stroke(); } } // 软边的椭圆：angle 是长轴的方向 function softEllipse(ctx, x, z, radiusLong, radiusShort, angle, channel, strength, core = 0.35) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, z); ctx.rotate(angle); ctx.scale(radiusLong, radiusShort); let gradient = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 0, core, 0, 0, 1); gradient.addColorStop(0,
); ctx.fillStyle = gradient; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); } let windAngle = Math.atan2(wind.z, wind.x); // ---- 人踩的小路和踩成一片的地方 ---- for (let path of FROST_GROUND.paths) { let length = 0; for (let i = 1; i < path.points.length; i++) length += Math.hypot(path.points[i][0] - path.points[i - 1][0], path.points[i][1] - path.points[i - 1][1]); softLine(a.ctx, frostSmoothLine(THREE, path.points, Math.max(4, Math.round(length / 0.7))), path.width * 1.3, RED, path.strength); } for (let [x, z, radius, strength] of FROST_GROUND.trampled) softEllipse(a.ctx, x, z, radius, radius, 0, RED, strength); // ---- 蓝冰：顺风拉长的椭圆 ---- for (let [x, z, radiusLong, radiusShort] of FROST_GROUND.ice) softEllipse(a.ctx, x, z, radiusLong, radiusShort, windAngle, GREEN, 1, 0.3); // ---- 车辆压实 ---- for (let [x, z, radius] of FROST_GROUND.parking) softEllipse(b.ctx, x, z, radius, radius, 0, BLUE, 0.9); // ---- 从碰撞盒自动算的三项 ---- let boxes = (G.physics && G.physics.boxes) || [], perpX = -wind.z, perpZ = wind.x; for (let box of boxes) { let x0 = box.min.x, x1 = box.max.x, z0 = box.min.z, z1 = box.max.z, width = x1 - x0, depth = z1 - z0, bottom = box.min.y, height = box.max.y - Math.max(0, bottom); if (box.max.y < 0.12 || width * depth > 600) continue; // 贴地的薄板（楼板、停机坪）不算 if (bottom < 0.45) { // 落地的东西：脚下一圈脏雪，越高的东西圈越宽 let spread = Math.min(1.6, Math.max(0.5, 0.4 + 0.18 * height)); a.ctx.fillStyle =
; for (let k of [1, 0.55, 0.15]) a.ctx.fillRect(x0 - spread * k, z0 - spread * k, width + 2 * spread * k, depth + 2 * spread * k); if (height < 0.5 || Math.max(width, depth) < 0.6) continue; // 背风的雪堆：从背风一侧的两个角往下风方向收成一个尖 let corners = [ [x0, z0], [x1, z0], [x0, z1], [x1, z1], ], across = corners.map(([x, z]) => x * perpX + z * perpZ), low = corners[across.indexOf(Math.min(...across))], high = corners[across.indexOf(Math.max(...across))], crosswind = Math.max(...across) - Math.min(...across), length = Math.min(10, Math.max(1.2, 1.6 * height + 0.5 * crosswind)), midX = (low[0] + high[0]) / 2, midZ = (low[1] + high[1]) / 2, tipX = midX + wind.x * (length + crosswind * 0.3), tipZ = midZ + wind.z * (length + crosswind * 0.3), gradient = b.ctx.createLinearGradient(midX, midZ, tipX, tipZ); gradient.addColorStop(0,
); b.ctx.fillStyle = gradient; b.ctx.beginPath(); b.ctx.moveTo(low[0], low[1]); b.ctx.lineTo(high[0], high[1]); b.ctx.lineTo(tipX, tipZ); b.ctx.closePath(); b.ctx.fill(); // 迎风一侧：风绕过去的地方雪被刮走，剩下发亮的硬壳 let scour = Math.min(1.2, Math.max(0.3, 0.25 * height)); b.ctx.fillStyle =
; for (let k of [1, 0.5]) b.ctx.fillRect(x0 - scour * k - wind.x * scour, z0 - scour * k - wind.z * scour, width + 2 * scour * k, depth + 2 * scour * k); } else if (bottom < 1.7 && width * depth > 1.5) { // 架空不高的东西（舱段、油罐）：下面晒不到也落不到新雪，是发灰的硬雪 a.ctx.fillStyle =
; for (let k of [0.2, -0.2]) a.ctx.fillRect(x0 - k, z0 - k, width + 2 * k, depth + 2 * k); } } let texture = (canvas) => { let t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas); t.flipY = false; t.colorSpace = THREE.NoColorSpace; t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.ClampToEdgeWrapping; t.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; t.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; t.generateMipmaps = true; t.anisotropy = 2; t.needsUpdate = true; return t; }; a.ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); return { maskA: texture(a.canvas), maskB: texture(b.canvas), pixelsA: a.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, size, size).data, size }; } // 贴地的吹雪：顺风拉长的丝，成团地来（阵风），整体顺风移动。地面的着色器和上面那层半透明的面片共用。 // 输入是风向坐标（沿风、横风，单位米）和时间，输出 0..1。 var FROST_WISP_GLSL =
本来该被抹平到什么程度
uniform float uTime, uMaskRes, uPixAngle, uSasScale; uniform sampler2D uMaskA, uMaskB, uDetail, uSlopeA, uSlopeB, uSastrugi; uniform vec3 uMaskRect, uSunDirW, uSunCol, uSlopeScale; uniform vec4 uWind, uPad, uTrackBox; uniform vec4 uTrackSeg[FG_SEGS]; uniform vec2 uTrackMeta[FG_SEGS]; varying vec3 vGWorld; float gRough; vec2 gNxz; float gSelf; float gGlint; float gSnowK; vec3 fgHash3(vec2 p){ vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * vec3(.1031, .1030, .0973)); q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33); return fract((q.xxy + q.yzz) * q.zyx); } float fgH1(vec2 p){ vec3 q = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * .1031); q += dot(q, q.yzx + 33.33); return fract((q.x + q.y) * q.z); } float fgVN(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f); return mix(mix(fgH1(i), fgH1(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), f.x), mix(fgH1(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), fgH1(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), f.x), f.y); } // 双三次取样：数据贴图只有 8 厘米一个像素，凑近了也不能看出像素格 vec4 fgBicubic(sampler2D t, vec2 uv, float res){ vec2 p = uv * res - 0.5; vec2 i = floor(p); vec2 f = p - i; vec2 f2 = f * f, f3 = f2 * f; vec2 w0 = (1.0 - 3.0 * f + 3.0 * f2 - f3) / 6.0, w1 = (4.0 - 6.0 * f2 + 3.0 * f3) / 6.0; vec2 w2 = (1.0 + 3.0 * f + 3.0 * f2 - 3.0 * f3) / 6.0, w3 = f3 / 6.0; vec2 g0 = w0 + w1, g1 = w2 + w3; vec2 h0 = (i + w1 / g0 - 0.5) / res, h1 = (i + w3 / g1 + 1.5) / res; return (textureLod(t, vec2(h0.x, h0.y), 0.0) * g0.x + textureLod(t, vec2(h1.x, h0.y), 0.0) * g1.x) * g0.y + (textureLod(t, vec2(h0.x, h1.y), 0.0) * g0.x + textureLod(t, vec2(h1.x, h1.y), 0.0) * g1.x) * g1.y; } // 一个标量在世界 xz 上的梯度，由它在屏幕上的差分换算。dxp、dyp 是世界 xz 在屏幕两个方向上的差分 vec2 fgGrad(float m, vec2 dxp, vec2 dyp){ float det = dxp.x * dyp.y - dxp.y * dyp.x; vec2 d = vec2(dFdx(m), dFdy(m)); return vec2(dyp.y * d.x - dxp.y * d.y, dxp.x * d.y - dyp.x * d.x) / (abs(det) > 1e-9 ? det : 1e-9); } // 履带沟的断面：r 是到沟中心的距离（米），返回高度（米）。沟底压下去 5 厘米，沟沿挤起一道 2 厘米的边 float fgRutH(float r){ return -0.05 * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.19, 0.27, r)) + 0.022 * exp(-pow((r - 0.335) / 0.055, 2.0)); } // 雪垄的高度（米），输入是风向坐标。两层大小不同的叠在一起 float fgSasH(vec2 wc){ return texture2D(uSastrugi, wc / vec2(16.0, 8.0)).r * 0.045 + texture2D(uSastrugi, wc / vec2(25.0, 12.0) + vec2(0.37, 0.61)).r * 0.08; } // 冰裂纹：到最近的格子边界的距离（格子单位） float fgCrack(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); float d1 = 9.0, d2 = 9.0; for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++) for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++){ vec2 o = vec2(float(x), float(y)); vec2 d = o + fgHash3(i + o).xy - f; float dd = dot(d, d); if (dd < d1){ d2 = d1; d1 = dd; } else if (dd < d2) d2 = dd; } return sqrt(d2) - sqrt(d1); } ${FROST_WISP_GLSL}
{ vec2 gxz = vGWorld.xz; vec3 gToCam = cameraPosition - vGWorld; float gDist = length(gToCam); float nearK = 1.0 - smoothstep(5.0, 18.0, gDist); float midK = 1.0 - smoothstep(30.0, 90.0, gDist); vec2 wc = vec2(dot(gxz, uWind.xy), dot(gxz, uWind.zw)); // 风向坐标：沿风、横风 vec2 dxp = dFdx(gxz), dyp = dFdy(gxz); float aaW = max(fwidth(gxz.x), fwidth(gxz.y)); // 一个像素在地面上有多宽（米） float onPad = step(uPad.w * 0.5, vGWorld.y); // 这块面片是不是停机坪的顶面 vec2 sunXZ = normalize(uSunDirW.xz); float tanE = uSunDirW.y / max(length(uSunDirW.xz), 1e-3); vec2 sunW = vec2(dot(sunXZ, uWind.xy), dot(sunXZ, uWind.zw)); // ---- 数据贴图 ---- vec2 muv = (gxz - uMaskRect.xy) * uMaskRect.z; float mIn = smoothstep(0.0, 0.03, min(min(muv.x, 1.0 - muv.x), min(muv.y, 1.0 - muv.y))); vec3 mA = fgBicubic(uMaskA, muv, uMaskRes).rgb * mIn; vec3 mB = texture2D(uMaskB, muv).rgb * mIn; // ---- 噪声：大（几十米）、中（几米）、小（一米以内）三档 ---- vec2 lowUv = wc * vec2(0.0071, 0.013) + vec2(0.13, 0.71); vec2 midUv = wc * vec2(0.043, 0.097) + vec2(0.37, 0.11); vec2 fineUv = gxz * 0.71 + vec2(0.11, 0.53); vec4 dLow = texture2D(uDetail, lowUv), dMid = texture2D(uDetail, midUv), dFine = texture2D(uDetail, fineUv); vec4 sLow = texture2D(uSlopeA, lowUv), sMid = texture2D(uSlopeA, midUv), sFineA = texture2D(uSlopeA, fineUv), sFineB = texture2D(uSlopeB, fineUv); // 风向不是处处一样：按大尺度噪声把横风坐标错开一些，雪垄和细条就不会全都平行地指向同一个消失点 vec2 wcs = wc + vec2(0.0, (dLow.g - 0.5) * 7.0 + (dMid.g - 0.5) * 0.8); float streak = texture2D(uDetail, wcs * vec2(0.031, 0.47) + 0.3).g; // 顺风的细条：长 8 米、宽半米 float refill = texture2D(uDetail, wc * vec2(0.03, 0.21) + 0.2).g; // 履带印被吹雪填平的段落 float iceFinger = texture2D(uDetail, wc * vec2(0.021, 0.19) + 0.5).g; // 伸进冰面的雪指 float n1 = fgVN(gxz * 1.9 + 3.0), n2 = fgVN(gxz * 6.1 + 17.0); float edgeN = n1 * 0.55 + n2 * 0.3 + dFine.r * 0.15; // 打散各种边界用 float wisp = fgWisp(uDetail, wc, uTime); float wispShade = fgWisp(uDetail, wc + sunW * 1.6, uTime); // 雪带离地三四十厘米，太阳又低，影子落在一米多以外 // ---- 分区 ---- float tramp = smoothstep(0.16, 0.6, mA.r + (edgeN - 0.5) * 0.4 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.2, mA.r)) * (1.0 - onPad); float grime = clamp(mA.b * 1.1 + (edgeN - 0.5) * 0.6 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.25, mA.b), 0.0, 1.0); float drift = smoothstep(0.08, 0.7, mB.r + (dMid.g - 0.5) * 0.35 * smoothstep(0.0, 0.2, mB.r)); float scour = smoothstep(0.2, 0.75, mB.g + (edgeN - 0.5) * 0.4 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.2, mB.g)) * (1.0 - drift); float park = smoothstep(0.2, 0.7, mB.b + (edgeN - 0.5) * 0.4 * smoothstep(0.02, 0.2, mB.b)) * (1.0 - onPad); // 冰：贴图里画的几块，加上可玩范围外按噪声零星露出的；边缘被顺风的雪指咬成锯齿 float iceBase = max(mA.g, smoothstep(0.8, 0.92, dLow.b * 0.65 + dMid.b * 0.35) * (1.0 - mIn)); float iceM = iceBase + ((iceFinger - 0.5) * 0.36 + (n2 - 0.5) * 0.12) * smoothstep(0.03, 0.3, iceBase); float ice = smoothstep(0.3, 0.46, iceM) * (1.0 - onPad); float iceRim = smoothstep(0.12, 0.3, iceM) * (1.0 - onPad) - ice; // 冰面外沿一圈：薄雪盖着冰，透出蓝色 // ---- 履带印（解析折线）---- float rut = 0.0, rutBar = 0.0, rutSh = 0.0, trk = 0.0, trkAlong = 0.0; vec2 rutSlope = vec2(0.0), trkT = vec2(1.0, 0.0); if (onPad < 0.5 && gxz.x > uTrackBox.x && gxz.y > uTrackBox.y && gxz.x < uTrackBox.z && gxz.y < uTrackBox.w) { float best = 1e9, bStr = 0.0; vec2 bD = vec2(0.0); for (int i = 0; i < FG_SEGS; i++) { vec4 sg = uTrackSeg[i]; vec2 pa = gxz - sg.xy, ba = sg.zw - sg.xy; float l2 = dot(ba, ba); float h = clamp(dot(pa, ba) / l2, 0.0, 1.0); vec2 dv = pa - ba * h; float d2 = dot(dv, dv); if (d2 < best) { best = d2; float len = sqrt(l2); trkAlong = uTrackMeta[i].x + h * len; bStr = uTrackMeta[i].y; trkT = ba / len; bD = dv; } } float td = sqrt(best); // 到履带印中心线的距离 if (td < 1.7) { vec2 nrm = bD / max(td, 1e-4); float str = bStr * (1.0 - 0.7 * smoothstep(0.42, 0.8, refill)) * (1.0 - ice * 0.85); float r = abs(td - 0.85) + (n2 - 0.5) * 0.03; // 两条沟的中心各在离中心线 0.85 米处 float h0 = fgRutH(r); rutSlope = nrm * ((fgRutH(r + 0.02) - fgRutH(r - 0.02)) / 0.04 * sign(td - 0.85) * str); rut = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.19, 0.27, r)) * str; float sAcross = dot(sunXZ, nrm); // 朝太阳一侧的沟壁把影子投在沟底 float rA = abs(td + sAcross * 0.16 - 0.85), rB = abs(td + sAcross * 0.34 - 0.85); rutSh = max(smoothstep(0.0, 0.012, fgRutH(rA) - h0 - 0.16 * tanE), smoothstep(0.0, 0.02, fgRutH(rB) - h0 - 0.34 * tanE)) * str; trk = (1.0 - smoothstep(1.05, 1.5, td)) * str; } } // 履刺压出来的横纹：每 17 厘米一道，太远了（一个像素装下不止一道）就不画 float barPh = trkAlong / 0.17; float barK = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.3, 0.9, fwidth(barPh))) * rut; float barTri = abs(fract(barPh) - 0.5) * 2.0; rutBar = barK * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.3, 0.6, barTri)) * (0.35 + 0.65 * n2); rutSlope += trkT * ((smoothstep(0.3, 0.6, barTri + 0.05) - smoothstep(0.3, 0.6, barTri - 0.05)) / 0.1 * (2.0 / 0.17) * sign(fract(barPh) - 0.5) * 0.007 * barK * (0.35 + 0.65 * n2)); // ---- 脚印：小路上每个 42 厘米的格子里随机踩一个，只在近处画 ---- float prints = 0.0; vec2 printSlope = vec2(0.0); if (tramp > 0.02 && nearK > 0.0) { vec2 fp = gxz / 0.42; vec2 fi = floor(fp); vec3 fh = fgHash3(fi); float fang = fh.z * 6.2832; vec2 fd = vec2(cos(fang), sin(fang)); vec2 q = (fp - fi - (0.3 + 0.4 * fh.xy)) * 0.42; vec2 ql = vec2(dot(q, fd), dot(q, vec2(-fd.y, fd.x))); float e = length(ql / vec2(0.15, 0.058)); float on = step(0.3, fract(fh.x * 7.3 + fh.y * 3.1)) * tramp * nearK * (1.0 - 0.6 * smoothstep(0.8, 1.0, tramp)); prints = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.7, 1.05, e)) * on; vec2 ge = vec2(ql.x / 0.0225, ql.y / 0.003364) / max(e, 1e-3); float dpr = -(smoothstep(0.7, 1.05, e + 0.03) - smoothstep(0.7, 1.05, e - 0.03)) / 0.06; printSlope = -(fd * ge.x + vec2(-fd.y, fd.x) * ge.y) * (dpr * 0.022 * on); } // ---- 停机坪：硬地面、标线、残雪 ---- vec3 hardAlb = vec3(0.1); float hardRough = 0.8, padSnow = 1.0; vec2 pp = gxz - uPad.xy; vec2 bq = abs(pp) - uPad.z; float bOut = length(max(bq, 0.0)) + min(max(bq.x, bq.y), 0.0); // 到停机坪边缘的距离，外面为正 if (onPad > 0.5) { float edge = -bOut; hardAlb = vec3(0.085, 0.088, 0.092) * (0.7 + 0.6 * dFine.r) * (0.85 + 0.3 * dMid.a); hardAlb += vec3(0.07, 0.068, 0.06) * smoothstep(0.62, 0.8, dFine.a); // 浅色的石子 vec2 sj = (0.5 - abs(fract(pp / 3.0 + 0.5) - 0.5)) * 3.0; // 3 米一块的板缝 float seam = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.012, 0.012 + aaW, min(sj.x, sj.y))) * clamp(0.03 / (aaW + 1e-4), 0.0, 1.0); hardAlb *= 1.0 - seam * 0.6; float aa = aaW * 0.75 + 1e-4; float ring = 1.0 - smoothstep(-aa, aa, abs(length(pp) - 4.35) - 0.17); // 圆圈 vec2 ha = abs(pp); float hMark = 1.0 - smoothstep(-aa, aa, min(max(abs(ha.x - 0.82) - 0.22, ha.y - 1.6), max(ha.x - 0.82, ha.y - 0.22))); // 字母 H float border = 1.0 - smoothstep(-aa, aa, abs(edge - 0.5) - 0.06); // 边线 float chipN = fgVN(gxz * 9.0) * 0.5 + fgVN(gxz * 31.0 + 5.0) * 0.3 + dFine.r * 0.2; float keep = smoothstep(0.3, 0.42, chipN + (dMid.g - 0.5) * 0.3); // 漆面磨掉的地方 hardAlb = mix(hardAlb, vec3(0.72, 0.52, 0.06), max(ring, border) * keep * 0.94); hardAlb = mix(hardAlb, vec3(0.74, 0.74, 0.7), hMark * keep * 0.94); float glaze = smoothstep(0.55, 0.75, dMid.g + (n1 - 0.5) * 0.3); // 结了一层薄冰的地方 hardRough = mix(0.8 - max(max(ring, border), hMark) * keep * 0.12 + (dFine.r - 0.5) * 0.2, 0.26, glaze * 0.7); hardAlb *= 1.0 - glaze * 0.25; // 残雪：四边往里堆的雪，加上顺风扫过来的雪条 float edgeSnow = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.1, 0.5 + 1.4 * n1, edge); float cover = smoothstep(0.62, 0.8, streak * 0.75 + n2 * 0.25 + edgeSnow * 0.3); padSnow = clamp(max(cover * 0.9, smoothstep(0.3, 0.55, edgeSnow + (n2 - 0.5) * 0.5)), 0.0, 1.0); } // 停机坪四周清出来的雪堆 float bermW = 0.7 + 0.7 * n1; float bermK = (1.0 - onPad) * (1.0 - max(tramp, trk)) * step(-0.2, bOut) * step(bOut, 3.5); float berm = smoothstep(0.0, 0.35, bOut) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.35, 0.35 + bermW * 1.6, bOut)) * bermK; float bermD = ((smoothstep(0.0, 0.35, bOut + 0.03) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.35, 0.35 + bermW * 1.6, bOut + 0.03))) - (smoothstep(0.0, 0.35, bOut - 0.03) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.35, 0.35 + bermW * 1.6, bOut - 0.03)))) / 0.06; vec2 bermDir = normalize(max(bq, 0.0) + 1e-5) * sign(pp); vec2 bermSlope = bermDir * (bermD * 0.42 * (0.6 + 0.8 * dFine.g) * bermK); // ---- 起伏：坡度先在风向坐标里累加，再转回世界 xz ---- float sasK = (0.14 + 0.86 * smoothstep(0.34, 0.66, dLow.b)) * (1.0 - drift) * (1.0 - tramp) * (1.0 - park * 0.8) * (1.0 - ice) * (1.0 - berm); vec4 sg1 = texture2D(uSastrugi, wcs / vec2(16.0, 8.0)), sg2 = texture2D(uSastrugi, wcs / vec2(25.0, 12.0) + vec2(0.37, 0.61)); vec2 sw = (0.5 - sLow.rg) * uSlopeScale.x * vec2(0.0071, 0.013) * 0.6; // 几十米长的缓丘 sw += (0.5 - sMid.rg) * uSlopeScale.x * vec2(0.043, 0.097) * 0.07; // 几米的小起伏 sw += ((0.5 - sg1.gb) * 0.045 + (0.5 - sg2.gb) * vec2(16.0 / 25.0, 8.0 / 12.0) * 0.08) * uSasScale * sasK; // 雪垄 float ripPh = wcs.x * 27.0 + (dMid.g - 0.5) * 10.0 + dFine.g * 2.0; // 横在风向上的细波纹，波长 23 厘米 float ripK = smoothstep(0.45, 0.7, dMid.a) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.5, 1.5, fwidth(ripPh))) * sasK; sw.x += cos(ripPh) * 27.0 * 0.0028 * ripK; vec2 slope = uWind.xy * sw.x + uWind.zw * sw.y; slope += (0.5 - sFineB.rg) * uSlopeScale.z * 0.71 * 0.0015 * nearK; // 颗粒 slope += (0.5 - sFineA.ba) * uSlopeScale.y * 0.71 * 0.012 * clamp(0.25 + tramp + park + berm * 2.0 + grime * 0.5, 0.0, 2.0); // 踩过、压过、堆过的地方疙疙瘩瘩 slope += clamp(fgGrad(mB.r, dxp, dyp), vec2(-1.5), vec2(1.5)) * 0.15 * midK; // 背风的雪堆是鼓起来的 slope -= clamp(fgGrad(tramp, dxp, dyp), vec2(-4.0), vec2(4.0)) * 0.06 * midK; // 小路踩低了一点 slope += rutSlope + printSlope + bermSlope; // 自阴影：朝太阳方向看过去，那边的雪垄够不够高挡住这里 float sH = sg1.r * 0.045 + sg2.r * 0.08; float sasSh = max(smoothstep(0.0, 0.015, fgSasH(wcs + sunW * 0.12) - sH - 0.12 * tanE), smoothstep(0.0, 0.025, fgSasH(wcs + sunW * 0.3) - sH - 0.3 * tanE)) * sasK; // ---- 雪的颜色和粗糙度 ---- float softN = dLow.g + (dMid.a - 0.5) * 0.22; float soft = smoothstep(0.5, 0.72, softN), crust = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.3, 0.5, softN); // 松一些的落雪区、发亮的风壳区 vec3 snow = vec3(0.845, 0.875, 0.915); snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.92, 0.935, 0.955), soft * 0.8); snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.78, 0.83, 0.9), crust * 0.55); snow *= 0.93 + 0.14 * streak + 0.05 * (dFine.r - 0.5); snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.91, 0.93, 0.955), max(drift * 0.8, berm * 0.6)); snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.7, 0.78, 0.88), scour * 0.5); vec3 packed = vec3(0.6, 0.645, 0.71) * (0.9 + 0.2 * dFine.r); snow *= 1.0 + 0.07 * smoothstep(0.04, 0.3, tramp) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.3, 0.7, tramp)); // 路边踢出来的松雪 snow = mix(snow, packed, max(tramp * 0.9, park * 0.7)); snow *= 1.0 - prints * 0.12; snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.66, 0.72, 0.8), max(rut * 0.7, trk * 0.18)); snow *= 1.0 - rutBar * 0.12; vec3 dirty = mix(vec3(0.52, 0.48, 0.42), vec3(0.38, 0.33, 0.27), dMid.a); snow = mix(snow, dirty, grime * (0.52 + 0.42 * edgeN)); vec2 gritP = gxz * 38.0; vec3 gritH = fgHash3(floor(gritP)); // 砂砾点：每格一粒，大小不一 snow *= 1.0 - (1.0 - smoothstep(0.1, 0.22, length(fract(gritP) - 0.25 - 0.5 * gritH.xy) / (0.4 + gritH.z))) * step(0.72, gritH.z) * grime * 0.5 * nearK; float oil = smoothstep(0.74, 0.84, fgVN(gxz * 2.3 + 7.0) * 0.7 + n2 * 0.3) * park; snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.09, 0.08, 0.07), oil * 0.7); snow = mix(snow, vec3(0.7, 0.82, 0.92), iceRim * 0.55); float rough = 0.82 + (dFine.r - 0.5) * 0.14 + soft * 0.12 - crust * 0.16 - scour * 0.2; rough = mix(rough, 0.56, max(tramp * 0.8, park * 0.7)); rough = mix(rough, 0.52, rut * 0.7); rough = mix(rough, 0.4, max(iceRim * 0.6, oil)); // ---- 冰 ---- vec3 iceC = vec3(0.3, 0.5, 0.7); float iceR = 0.16; if (ice > 0.01) { vec2 cp = gxz + (vec2(n1, n2) - 0.5) * 0.5; float ck = fgCrack(cp * 0.31), ck2 = fgCrack(cp * 0.9 + 7.0); // 3 米和 1 米两级裂纹 float cw = 0.012 + aaW * 0.8, cdim = clamp(0.03 / (aaW + 1e-4), 0.0, 1.0); // 线宽（米），远了变宽变淡 float crack = max(1.0 - smoothstep(cw * 0.1, cw * 0.31, ck), (1.0 - smoothstep(cw * 0.3, cw * 0.9, ck2)) * 0.4 * step(0.5, n1)) * cdim; float halo = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.1, ck); // 裂纹一侧发白的裂面 float milky = smoothstep(0.55, 0.8, dMid.a); // 乳白的气泡层 iceC = mix(vec3(0.2, 0.4, 0.62), vec3(0.4, 0.6, 0.78), dMid.g * 0.7 + dLow.g * 0.3); // 蓝冰其实很亮，是浅蓝不是藏青 iceC = mix(iceC, vec3(0.62, 0.75, 0.85), milky * 0.4); iceC = mix(iceC, vec3(0.86, 0.93, 0.98), clamp(halo * 0.12 + crack * 0.85, 0.0, 1.0)); iceR = 0.16 + crack * 0.25 + milky * 0.1; } float iceK = ice * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.58, 0.78, streak + (dFine.g - 0.5) * 0.3) * 0.85); // 冰面上贴着顺风的雪条 // ---- 合成 ---- float snowAmt = mix(1.0, padSnow, onPad); vec3 alb = mix(mix(hardAlb, snow, snowAmt), iceC, iceK); rough = mix(mix(hardRough, rough, snowAmt), iceR, iceK); slope *= mix(1.0, 0.3 * padSnow, onPad) * (1.0 - iceK * 0.92); slope += (0.5 - sMid.ba) * uSlopeScale.y * 0.097 * 0.004 * iceK; // 冰面自己的一点波动 // 贴地的吹雪扫过：一层白纱，盖住下面的明暗 float veil = wisp * 0.45; alb = mix(alb, vec3(0.92, 0.94, 0.965), veil); rough = mix(rough, 0.95, veil); slope *= 1.0 - veil * 0.7; rough += smoothstep(40.0, 220.0, gDist) * 0.06; diffuseColor.rgb *= alb; gRough = clamp(rough, 0.05, 1.0); gNxz = slope; gSnowK = mix(snowAmt * (1.0 - iceK), 1.0, veil); gSelf = 1.0 - clamp(max(sasSh * 0.55 * midK, rutSh * 0.8) * (1.0 - veil), 0.0, 1.0) * mix(1.0, padSnow, onPad); gSelf *= 1.0 - 0.25 * wispShade * (1.0 - veil); // ---- 雪粒的闪光：把雪面分成和像素差不多大的小格，每格一个随机朝向的晶面，朝向刚好把太阳反射进眼睛的才亮 ---- gGlint = 0.0; float glK = gSnowK * (1.0 - smoothstep(70.0, 160.0, gDist)) * (1.0 - tramp * 0.6) * (1.0 - grime) * (1.0 - veil); if (glK > 0.01) { vec3 gH = normalize(gToCam / gDist + uSunDirW); float lvl = log2(max(gDist * uPixAngle * 2.4, 0.003) / 0.003); float l0 = floor(lvl), lf = lvl - l0; for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) { float cell = 0.003 * exp2(l0 + float(k)); vec3 hh = fgHash3(floor(gxz / cell) + (l0 + float(k)) * 19.7); float tilt = hh.x * hh.x * 2.6, az = hh.y * 6.2832; vec3 fn = normalize(vec3(cos(az) * tilt, 1.0, sin(az) * tilt)); gGlint += smoothstep(0.996, 0.9994, dot(fn, gH)) * step(0.4, hh.z) * (k == 0 ? 1.0 - lf : lf); } gGlint *= glK; } }
实际的直射漫反射 ÷ 没有影子时应有的值
; // ---- 贴图 ---- // N1 是港口地面模块里
的函数 let detailSize = highQuality ? 512 : 256, detailMaps = bakeFrostDetailMaps(detailSize), sastrugi = bakeFrostSastrugi(512, 16, 8), detail = N1(detailMaps.detail, detailSize, { aniso: anisotropy }), slopeA = N1(detailMaps.slopeA, detailSize, { aniso: anisotropy }), slopeB = N1(detailMaps.slopeB, detailSize, { aniso: anisotropy }), sastrugiMap = N1(sastrugi.data, 512, { aniso: anisotropy }), masks = drawFrostGroundMasks(G, 1024); // ---- 俯视高度图：积雪和吹雪层用它判断头顶有没有东西 ---- let topTarget = null; try { topTarget = renderFrostSnowTopMap(G, rect, 1024); } catch (error) { console.error(

} // ---- 履带印的折线段 ---- let segmentCount = FROST_GROUND.segments, trackSegments = [], trackMeta = [], trackBox = [Infinity, Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity]; for (let track of FROST_GROUND.tracks) { let points = frostSmoothLine(THREE, track.points, track.steps), travelled = 0; travelled += Math.hypot(bx - ax, bz - az); trackBox[0] = Math.min(trackBox[0], ax, bx); trackBox[1] = Math.min(trackBox[1], az, bz); trackBox[2] = Math.max(trackBox[2], ax, bx); trackBox[3] = Math.max(trackBox[3], az, bz); } } while (trackSegments.length < segmentCount) { trackSegments.push(new THREE.Vector4(1e5, 1e5, 1e5 + 1, 1e5)); // 用不到的位置放一段很远的线 trackMeta.push(new THREE.Vector2(0, 0)); } // ---- 地面的材质 ---- let sun = G.render && G.render.sun, uniforms = { uTime: { value: 0 }, uMaskRes: { value: masks.size }, uPixAngle: { value: 0.002 }, uSasScale: { value: sastrugi.scale }, uMaskA: { value: masks.maskA }, uMaskB: { value: masks.maskB }, uDetail: { value: detail }, uSlopeA: { value: slopeA }, uSlopeB: { value: slopeB }, uSastrugi: { value: sastrugiMap }, uMaskRect: { value: new THREE.Vector3(rect.x0, rect.z0, 1 / rect.size) }, uSunDirW: { value: Mn.dir.clone() }, uSunCol: { value: new THREE.Color().copy(Mn.color).multiplyScalar(Mn.intensity) }, uSlopeScale: { value: new THREE.Vector3(...detailMaps.scale) }, uWind: { value: new THREE.Vector4(wind.x, wind.z, -wind.z, wind.x) }, uPad: { value: new THREE.Vector4(pad.x, pad.z, pad.half, pad.top) }, uTrackBox: { value: new THREE.Vector4(trackBox[0] - 2, trackBox[1] - 2, trackBox[2] + 2, trackBox[3] + 2) }, uTrackSeg: { value: trackSegments }, uTrackMeta: { value: trackMeta }, }, material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, roughness: 1, metalness: 0 }); uniform sampler2D uDetail, uTopMap; uniform float uTime, uAlpha, uCap, uShift, uTopOn; uniform vec4 uWind; uniform vec3 uAmbient, uSunCol, uSunDirW, uMaskRect; varying vec3 vW; ${FROST_WISP_GLSL} // 俯视高度图上这个位置最高的表面有多高 float topAt(vec2 p){ vec2 muv = (p - uMaskRect.xy) * uMaskRect.z; return textureLod(uTopMap, vec2(muv.x, 1.0 - muv.y), 0.0).r * uTopOn * step(0.0, muv.x) * step(muv.x, 1.0) * step(0.0, muv.y) * step(muv.y, 1.0); } void main(){ vec2 wc = vec2(dot(vW.xz, uWind.xy), dot(vW.xz, uWind.zw)); vec3 toCam = cameraPosition - vW; float dist = length(toCam); vec3 view = toCam / dist; // 这一层很薄：视线越贴着地面，穿过它的路程越长，看起来越浓；从上往下看就淡得多。uAlpha 是垂直穿过时的光学厚度 float steep = abs(view.y); float wisp = fgWisp(uDetail, wc + uShift, uTime); float density = wisp * wisp; // 带的中间浓、边上很快变稀：贴着地面看过去才是一条条的，不会连成一整片 float a = min(1.0 - exp(-density * uAlpha * (1.0 - 0.7 * smoothstep(0.3, 0.9, steep)) / max(steep, 0.02)), uCap); // 脚边不画；远处按道理会连成一片白，这里压掉一半，免得把远处的地面全盖住 a *= smoothstep(2.0, 7.0, dist) * mix(1.0, 0.5, smoothstep(30.0, 90.0, dist)) * (1.0 - smoothstep(120.0, 230.0, dist)); a *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.05, 0.6, topAt(vW.xz) - vW.y); // 头顶有东西的地方没有 if (a < 0.004) discard; // 有没有被建筑的影子罩住：朝太阳的方向在高度图上探几步 vec2 sunXZ = normalize(uSunDirW.xz); float tanE = uSunDirW.y / max(length(uSunDirW.xz), 1e-3); float lit = 1.0; lit *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.4, topAt(vW.xz + sunXZ * 1.5) - vW.y - 1.5 * tanE); lit *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.5, topAt(vW.xz + sunXZ * 4.0) - vW.y - 4.0 * tanE); lit *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.6, topAt(vW.xz + sunXZ * 9.0) - vW.y - 9.0 * tanE); lit *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, topAt(vW.xz + sunXZ * 18.0) - vW.y - 18.0 * tanE); lit *= 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, topAt(vW.xz + sunXZ * 32.0) - vW.y - 32.0 * tanE); float phase = pow(max(dot(-view, uSunDirW), 0.0), 8.0); // 冰晶把光往前散射：迎着太阳看时亮得多 gl_FragColor = vec4(uAmbient + uSunCol * (lit * (0.6 + 2.2 * phase)), a); }
; layer.position.y = oi.groundY + height; layer.renderOrder = 880; layer.castShadow = layer.receiveShadow = false; layer.frustumCulled = false; layer.matrixAutoUpdate = false; layer.updateMatrix(); root.add(layer); return layer; }); G.scene.add(root); // ---- 给别的模块查询用 ---- let insidePad = (x, z) => Math.abs(x - pad.x) < pad.half && Math.abs(z - pad.z) < pad.half, maskAt = (x, z, channel) => { let u = Math.floor(((x - rect.x0) / rect.size) * masks.size), v = Math.floor(((z - rect.z0) / rect.size) * masks.size); if (u < 0 || v < 0 || u >= masks.size || v >= masks.size) return 0; return masks.pixelsA[(v * masks.size + u) * 4 + channel] / 255; }, time = 0, resolution = new THREE.Vector2(); return { root, apron: plane, padTop, blowingSnow: wispLayers, uniforms, topMap: topTarget, // 风吹去的方向（世界坐标的单位向量），天气粒子可以用它保持一致 wind: new THREE.Vector3(wind.x, 0, wind.z), // 脚下是什么：

, windGain: 1.7, // 这张图自带的声音，格式和 10-audio.js 的音效表 Go 一样 sounds: { wind_polar: { gen: synthFrostWind, n: 1, sr: 22050, peak: 0.5, loop: 1 } }, }, }); // 多层寻路。 // // 04-physics.js 里原有的寻路是一张二维网格，路径点高度恒为 0，只够港口图用。 // 这里另做一套
的：一张地图登记若干层（每层一个标高）和层与层之间的连接（梯道）， // 寻路结果的路径点带高度，梯道上的路径点另带梯道的信息。 // // 地图怎么登记：在注册表里给地图加一个 nav 字段， // gameMaps.某地图.nav = (G, level) => ({ // layers: [{ name:
室外钢梯下段
, points: [[x, z], [x, z], ...] }, ...], // seed: [x, y, z], // 一个肯定走得到的位置（不给就用玩家出生点），从它出发走不到的格子一律算不可走 // }) // 物理模块在 build() 时调用 installLayeredNav；地图没有 nav 字段就什么都不做，寻路仍走原来的单层网格。 // // 层： // - 每层一张网格，格子边长 0.25 米，范围和单层网格相同。 // - 除了标了 ground 的那一层，格子必须有楼板托着才可走：楼板是顶面在 [y - 0.06, y + 0.47] 之间的碰撞盒。 // 没有楼板的地方（楼梯井、平台外面）当成障碍。 // - 障碍的规则和单层网格一致，只是换算到本层的标高：顶面高出本层不到 0.47 米的盒子可以直接踩上去， // 底面高出本层超过 1.5 米的盒子碰不到，其余都是障碍。 // - 障碍和楼板边缘都向外扩 radius（0.5 米）。和单层网格不同的是格子更细、外扩是精确的， // 所以 2 米宽的门洞还剩 1 米宽、至少 3 格可走，不会被堵死。 // // 梯道（link）： // - 平面上的一条折线，从 from 层的一个可走格子走到 to 层的一个可走格子。机器人在梯道上严格沿折线走， // 不查网格，所以 1 米多宽的梯道也能通过。折线要画在梯道的中线上，拐弯处用 navRoundCorners 倒成圆弧。 // - 高度不用登记：建的时候沿折线每 4 厘米向下探一次碰撞盒，得到台阶的实际高度（raw）， // 再做一次滑动平均得到坡面的高度（smooth）。台阶改了级数或尺寸，只要平面位置不变就不用改登记。 // - 探的过程中顺便检查：相邻两次的落差不能超过 0.45 米，终点的高度要和 to 层对得上， // 两头必须落在可走的格子里。不满足的梯道记进 problems，并且不参与寻路。 // // 留空： // - 可走的格子按离障碍的远近分三档（margin）：2 是紧挨着障碍格，1 是隔一格，0 是更远。 // 寻路时走 margin 高的格子代价更高，拉直路径时也不允许抄近路抄到比原路更贴墙的格子里。 // 结果是路径在走廊和门洞里走中线，拐弯的地方尽量离墙 1 米以上（机头在前 0.76 米，原地转身需要这么大的地方）。 // - 另有一张 solid 表：障碍只向外扩 0.16 米（机头的半径）。机器人用它检查机头有没有顶到墙。 // // 寻路： // - 同一层：加权 A*。 // - 跨层：每个梯道口预先算好一张本层的距离场。先在
组成的小图上找出走哪几条梯道最近， // 再顺着距离场把每一段的格子路径取出来，不用再搜索。 // - 路径是 THREE.Vector3 的数组，数组上有 layered = true。梯道上的点另有 link（梯道）、dir（1 正向 / -1 反向）、 // s（沿梯道的距离）三个字段。 var NAV_LAYERED = { cell: 0.25, // 格子边长 radius: 0.5, // 障碍和楼板边缘向外扩的距离（和单层网格的 yf 相同） headRadius: 0.16, // solid 表里障碍向外扩的距离 marginCost: [1, 1.6, 3], // 走进 margin 为 0、1、2 的格子，代价各是距离的多少倍 stepUp: 0.47, // 顶面高出本层不到这个值的盒子可以踩上去（和单层网格的 u1 + 0.05 相同） bodyHeight: 1.5, // 底面高出本层超过这个值的盒子不算障碍（和单层网格相同） floorBelow: 0.06, // 楼板顶面最多可以比层的标高低这么多 maxExpand: 24000, // A* 最多展开多少个格子 heuristicWeight: 1.2, // A* 估价的权重，大于 1 时搜索更快，路径略长（之后还会拉直） smoothWindow: 48, // 拉直路径时最多往前看多少个格子 snapRadius: 3, // 位置不在可走格子上时，在这个半径内找最近的可走格子 linkSample: 0.04, // 沿梯道探高度的间距 linkSmooth: 0.28, // 坡面高度的滑动平均半宽 linkMaxStep: 0.45, // 梯道上相邻两次探到的高度最多差这么多 linkPenalty: 1.3, // 梯道的代价是它长度的多少倍（走楼梯比走平地慢） linkSpeed: 1.2, // 机器人在梯道上的最高速度 }; // 把折线的拐角倒成圆弧。points 是 [[x, z], ...]，radius 是圆弧半径，每段圆弧按不超过 maxStep 弧度切成小段。 // 圆弧放不下（相邻的边太短）时自动缩小半径。 function navRoundCorners(points, radius, maxStep = 0.26) { let out = [points[0]]; for (let i = 1; i < points.length - 1; i++) { let [px, pz] = points[i - 1], [cx, cz] = points[i], [nx, nz] = points[i + 1], inLength = Math.hypot(cx - px, cz - pz), outLength = Math.hypot(nx - cx, nz - cz), ix = (cx - px) / inLength, iz = (cz - pz) / inLength, ox = (nx - cx) / outLength, oz = (nz - cz) / outLength, turn = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ix * ox + iz * oz))); if (turn < 0.05) { out.push(points[i]); continue; } // 圆弧在两条边上各占 radius * tan(turn / 2)，最多占到相邻边的一半（首尾两条边可以占满） let inRoom = i === 1 ? inLength : inLength / 2, outRoom = i === points.length - 2 ? outLength : outLength / 2, cut = Math.min(radius * Math.tan(turn / 2), inRoom, outRoom), r = cut / Math.tan(turn / 2), side = ix * oz - iz * ox > 0 ? 1 : -1, // 圆心在行进方向的哪一侧 startX = cx - ix * cut, startZ = cz - iz * cut, centerX = startX - iz * side * r, centerZ = startZ + ix * side * r, startAngle = Math.atan2(startZ - centerZ, startX - centerX), steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(turn / maxStep)); for (let k = 0; k <= steps; k++) { let angle = startAngle + (side * turn * k) / steps; out.push([centerX + Math.cos(angle) * r, centerZ + Math.sin(angle) * r]); } } out.push(points[points.length - 1]); return out; } function createLayeredNav(G, physics, definition) { let THREE = G.THREE, settings = { ...NAV_LAYERED, ...(definition.settings || {}) }, cell = settings.cell, minX = Ya, minZ = ol, width = Math.round((EA - Ya) / cell), height = Math.round((SA - ol) / cell), count = width * height, reach = Math.ceil(settings.radius / cell - 1e-6), layers = definition.layers.map((layer, index) => ({ index, name: layer.name, y: layer.y, ground: !!layer.ground, areas: layer.areas || null, blocked: new Uint8Array(count), // 0 可走，1 障碍或没有楼板，2 从 seed 走不到 margin: new Uint8Array(count), // 可走格子离障碍的远近：2 紧挨着，1 隔一格，0 更远 solid: new Uint8Array(count), // 障碍本身（只扩了机头的半径） free: 0, })), links = [], problems = [], // 搜索用的临时数组，所有层共用 cost = new Float64Array(count), parent = new Int32Array(count), opened = new Uint32Array(count), closed = new Uint32Array(count), stamp = 0, heapCells = [], heapKeys = [], stepI = [1, -1, 0, 0, 1, 1, -1, -1], stepJ = [0, 0, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1, -1], scratch = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, nav; let cellAt = (x, z) => { let i = Math.floor((x - minX) / cell), j = Math.floor((z - minZ) / cell); return i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= width || j >= height ? -1 : j * width + i; }, cellX = (c) => minX + ((c % width) + 0.5) * cell, cellZ = (c) => minZ + (Math.floor(c / width) + 0.5) * cell, layerByName = (name) => layers.find((layer) => layer.name === name) || null; function heapPush(c, key) { let at = heapCells.length; for (heapCells.push(c), heapKeys.push(key); at > 0; ) { let up = (at - 1) >> 1; if (heapKeys[up] <= key) break; heapCells[at] = heapCells[up]; heapKeys[at] = heapKeys[up]; at = up; } heapCells[at] = c; heapKeys[at] = key; } function heapPop() { let top = heapCells[0], c = heapCells.pop(), key = heapKeys.pop(), size = heapCells.length; if (size) { let at = 0; for (;;) { let down = 2 * at + 1; if (down >= size) break; if (down + 1 < size && heapKeys[down + 1] < heapKeys[down]) down++; if (heapKeys[down] >= key) break; heapCells[at] = heapCells[down]; heapKeys[at] = heapKeys[down]; at = down; } heapCells[at] = c; heapKeys[at] = key; } return top; } // ---------------- 建网格 ---------------- // 把 source 里非 0 的格子向四周扩 reach 格，结果或进 target function dilate(source, target, reach) { let rows = new Uint8Array(count); for (let j = 0; j < height; j++) { let row = j * width, last = -1e9; for (let i = 0; i < width; i++) { if (source[row + i]) last = i; if (i - last <= reach) rows[row + i] = 1; } last = 1e9; for (let i = width - 1; i >= 0; i--) { if (source[row + i]) last = i; if (last - i <= reach) rows[row + i] = 1; } } for (let i = 0; i < width; i++) { let last = -1e9; for (let j = 0; j < height; j++) { if (rows[j * width + i]) last = j; if (j - last <= reach) target[j * width + i] = 1; } last = 1e9; for (let j = height - 1; j >= 0; j--) { if (rows[j * width + i]) last = j; if (last - j <= reach) target[j * width + i] = 1; } } } function buildLayer(layer) { let blocked = layer.blocked, y = layer.y, boxes = physics.boxes, r = settings.radius, eps = 1e-4; blocked.fill(0); layer.solid.fill(0); // (1) 楼板。在格子的角点上采样：四个角都有楼板托着的格子才算有楼板。 // 这样相邻的两块楼板拼缝处不会断开，而宽过一格的洞一定会被发现。 if (!layer.ground) { let latticeWidth = width + 1, lattice = new Uint8Array(latticeWidth * (height + 1)), hollow = new Uint8Array(count); for (let box of boxes) { if (box.bulletOnly || box.max.y < y - settings.floorBelow || box.max.y > y + settings.stepUp) continue; let i0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((box.min.x - eps - minX) / cell)), i1 = Math.min(width, Math.floor((box.max.x + eps - minX) / cell)), j0 = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((box.min.z - eps - minZ) / cell)), j1 = Math.min(height, Math.floor((box.max.z + eps - minZ) / cell)); if (i1 < i0) continue; for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) lattice.fill(1, j * latticeWidth + i0, j * latticeWidth + i1 + 1); } for (let j = 0; j < height; j++) for (let i = 0; i < width; i++) { let at = j * latticeWidth + i; if (!(lattice[at] && lattice[at + 1] && lattice[at + latticeWidth] && lattice[at + latticeWidth + 1])) hollow[j * width + i] = 1; } dilate(hollow, blocked, reach); } // (2) 登记了范围的层，范围以外不可走 if (layer.areas) for (let c = 0; c < count; c++) { let x = cellX(c), z = cellZ(c); if (!layer.areas.some((a) => x >= a[0] && z >= a[1] && x <= a[2] && z <= a[3])) blocked[c] = 1; } // (3) 障碍，向外扩 radius。凡是和扩大后的盒子有重叠的格子都算障碍，所以可走格子里的任何一点离障碍都不小于 radius。 for (let box of boxes) { if (box.noNav || box.bulletOnly || box.max.y < y + settings.stepUp || box.min.y > y + settings.bodyHeight) continue; let i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((box.min.x - r - minX) / cell)), i1 = Math.min(width - 1, Math.floor((box.max.x + r - eps - minX) / cell)), j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((box.min.z - r - minZ) / cell)), j1 = Math.min(height - 1, Math.floor((box.max.z + r - eps - minZ) / cell)); if (i1 < i0) continue; for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) blocked.fill(1, j * width + i0, j * width + i1 + 1); let h = settings.headRadius, a0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((box.min.x - h - minX) / cell)), a1 = Math.min(width - 1, Math.floor((box.max.x + h - eps - minX) / cell)), b0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((box.min.z - h - minZ) / cell)), b1 = Math.min(height - 1, Math.floor((box.max.z + h - eps - minZ) / cell)); for (let j = b0; j <= b1; j++) layer.solid.fill(1, j * width + a0, j * width + a1 + 1); } // (4) 网格的边缘，以及海（x 大于 quayX 的地方） for (let j = 0; j < height; j++) for (let i = 0; i < width; i++) if (i < reach || j < reach || i >= width - reach || j >= height - reach || minX + (i + 0.5) * cell > oi.quayX - r) blocked[j * width + i] = 1; } // 一条梯道。平面折线加上沿线探出来的高度。 function buildLink(spec) { let from = layerByName(spec.from), to = layerByName(spec.to), points = spec.points, last = points.length - 1, at = [0], // 每个折点沿梯道的距离 note = (text) => problems.push(
); for (let i = 1; i <= last; i++) at.push(at[i - 1] + Math.hypot(points[i][0] - points[i - 1][0], points[i][1] - points[i - 1][1])); let length = at[last], link = { name: spec.name, ok: true, from, to, points, at, length, cost: length * settings.linkPenalty, speed: spec.speed || settings.linkSpeed, a: null, b: null, raw: null, smooth: null, ds: 0, // s 处在第几段折线上 segmentAt(s) { let i = 0; while (i < last - 1 && at[i + 1] <= s) i++; return i; }, // s 处的位置。高度是坡面高度。 pointAt(s, out) { s = s < 0 ? 0 : s > length ? length : s; let i = link.segmentAt(s), t = (s - at[i]) / (at[i + 1] - at[i] || 1); out.x = points[i][0] + (points[i + 1][0] - points[i][0]) * t; out.z = points[i][1] + (points[i + 1][1] - points[i][1]) * t; out.y = link.heightAt(s); return out; }, // s 处的行进方向（正向），单位向量写进 out.x / out.z tangentAt(s, out) { s = s < 0 ? 0 : s > length ? length : s; let i = link.segmentAt(s), span = at[i + 1] - at[i] || 1; out.x = (points[i + 1][0] - points[i][0]) / span; out.z = (points[i + 1][1] - points[i][1]) / span; return out; }, // 坡面高度（平滑过的）和台阶的实际高度 heightAt: (s) => sample(link.smooth, s), rawHeightAt: (s) => link.raw[Math.max(0, Math.min(link.raw.length - 1, Math.round(s / link.ds)))], // 坡度：正向每走 1 米升高多少米 slopeAt: (s) => (sample(link.smooth, s + 0.2) - sample(link.smooth, s - 0.2)) / 0.4, }; function sample(values, s) { let f = Math.max(0, Math.min(values.length - 1, s / link.ds)), k = Math.min(values.length - 2, Math.floor(f)); return values[k] + (values[k + 1] - values[k]) * (f - k); } if (!from || !to) { note(
); link.ok = false; link.raw = link.smooth = new Float32Array(2); link.ds = length || 1; return link; } // 沿线探高度 let samples = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(length / settings.linkSample) + 1), ds = length / (samples - 1), raw = new Float32Array(samples), smooth = new Float32Array(samples), here = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }; link.ds = ds; link.raw = raw; link.smooth = raw; // pointAt 里要用，先指向 raw let h = physics.groundHeight(points[0][0], points[0][1], from.y + settings.stepUp); raw[0] = h; if (Math.abs(h - from.y) > settings.stepUp) { note(
在 ${(k * ds).toFixed(2)} 米处（${here.x.toFixed(2)}, ${here.z.toFixed(2)}）脚下落空，高度从 ${h.toFixed(2)} 掉到 ${next.toFixed(2)}
终点的高度是 ${h.toFixed(2)}，和 ${to.name} 层的 ${to.y} 对不上
端点 (${e.point.x.toFixed(2)}, ${e.point.z.toFixed(2)}) 不在 ${e.layer.name} 层的可走格子上
seed（玩家出生点）不在可走的格子上，没有剔除走不到的区域
梯道
：从出生点走不到它的任何一头
[nav] 多层寻路有问题：\n
)); } // ---------------- 查询 ---------------- // 离 (x, z) 最近的可走格子，radius 以内。没有返回 -1。 function nearestFree(layer, x, z, radius) { let blocked = layer.blocked, ci = Math.max(0, Math.min(width - 1, Math.floor((x - minX) / cell))), cj = Math.max(0, Math.min(height - 1, Math.floor((z - minZ) / cell))); if (!blocked[cj * width + ci]) return cj * width + ci; let rings = Math.ceil(radius / cell), best = -1, bestDistance = Infinity; for (let ring = 1; ring <= rings; ring++) { // 一圈上最近的点不一定比下一圈的近，所以找到之后再多看一圈 if (best >= 0 && (ring - 1) * cell > bestDistance) break; for (let dj = -ring; dj <= ring; dj++) for (let di = -ring; di <= ring; di++) { if (Math.max(Math.abs(di), Math.abs(dj)) !== ring) continue; let i = ci + di, j = cj + dj; if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= width || j >= height || blocked[j * width + i]) continue; let d = Math.hypot(minX + (i + 0.5) * cell - x, minZ + (j + 0.5) * cell - z); if (d < bestDistance) { bestDistance = d; best = j * width + i; } } } return best; } // 一个位置算在哪一层的哪个格子上。exact 为真表示它本来就站在这个可走格子里。 function locate(position) { let x = position.x, z = position.z, y = position.y || 0, home = cellAt(x, z), best = null, bestScore = Infinity; if (home >= 0) for (let layer of layers) if (!layer.blocked[home] && Math.abs(y - layer.y) <= 0.6) return { layer, cell: home, exact: true }; for (let layer of layers) { let found = nearestFree(layer, x, z, settings.snapRadius); if (found < 0) continue; let score = Math.hypot(cellX(found) - x, cellZ(found) - z) + Math.abs(y - layer.y) * 1.5; if (score < bestScore) { bestScore = score; best = { layer, cell: found, exact: found === home }; } } return best; } // 标高离 y 最近的层，差得超过 1 米就算不在任何一层上 function layerNear(y) { let best = null, bestGap = 1; for (let layer of layers) { let gap = Math.abs(y - layer.y); if (gap <= bestGap) { bestGap = gap; best = layer; } } return best; } // 两个格子之间的直线上有没有障碍（和单层网格的做法相同）。limit 是途中允许的最高 margin。 function lineClear(layer, from, to, limit) { let blocked = layer.blocked, margin = layer.margin, i = from % width, j = Math.floor(from / width), ti = to % width, tj = Math.floor(to / width), di = Math.abs(ti - i), dj = Math.abs(tj - j), si = i < ti ? 1 : -1, sj = j < tj ? 1 : -1, error = di - dj; for (let guard = di + dj + 2; guard > 0; guard--) { if (blocked[j * width + i] || margin[j * width + i] > limit) return false; if (i === ti && j === tj) return true; let twice = 2 * error; if (twice > -dj && twice < di) { if (blocked[j * width + i + si] || blocked[(j + sj) * width + i]) return false; if (margin[j * width + i + si] > limit || margin[(j + sj) * width + i] > limit) return false; error += di - dj; i += si; j += sj; } else if (twice > -dj) { error -= dj; i += si; } else { error += di; j += sj; } } return false; } // 同一层里的加权 A*。返回 { cells, cost（米）}，走不到或超出展开上限返回 null。 function searchLayer(layer, start, goal) { stamp++; heapCells.length = 0; heapKeys.length = 0; let blocked = layer.blocked, margin = layer.margin, weights = settings.marginCost, goalI = goal % width, goalJ = Math.floor(goal / width), weight = settings.heuristicWeight, estimate = (c) => { let di = Math.abs((c % width) - goalI), dj = Math.abs(Math.floor(c / width) - goalJ); return (di + dj - 0.5858 * Math.min(di, dj)) * weight; }, expanded = 0; cost[start] = 0; opened[start] = stamp; parent[start] = -1; heapPush(start, estimate(start)); while (heapCells.length) { let current = heapPop(); if (current === goal) break; if (closed[current] === stamp) continue; closed[current] = stamp; if (++expanded > settings.maxExpand) return null; let ci = current % width, cj = Math.floor(current / width); for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { let ni = ci + stepI[k], nj = cj + stepJ[k]; if (ni < 0 || nj < 0 || ni >= width || nj >= height) continue; let next = nj * width + ni; if (blocked[next] || closed[next] === stamp) continue; if (k >= 4 && (blocked[cj * width + ni] || blocked[nj * width + ci])) continue; let g = cost[current] + (k >= 4 ? 1.4142 : 1) * weights[margin[next]]; if (opened[next] !== stamp || g < cost[next]) { cost[next] = g; opened[next] = stamp; parent[next] = current; heapPush(next, g + estimate(next)); } } } if (opened[goal] !== stamp) return null; let cells = []; for (let c = goal; c !== -1; c = parent[c]) cells.push(c); cells.reverse(); nav.lastExpanded = expanded; return { cells, cost: cost[goal] * cell }; } // 顺着距离场从 from 走到距离场的源头，返回经过的格子（含两头） function descend(field, layer, from) { let blocked = layer.blocked; if (!(field[from] < Infinity)) return null; let cells = [from], current = from; for (let guard = count; field[current] > 0 && guard > 0; guard--) { let ci = current % width, cj = Math.floor(current / width), best = -1, bestValue = Infinity; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { let ni = ci + stepI[k], nj = cj + stepJ[k]; if (ni < 0 || nj < 0 || ni >= width || nj >= height) continue; let next = nj * width + ni; if (blocked[next] || (k >= 4 && (blocked[cj * width + ni] || blocked[nj * width + ci]))) continue; let value = field[next] + (k >= 4 ? 1.4142 : 1); if (value < bestValue) { bestValue = value; best = next; } } if (best < 0 || !(field[best] < field[current])) return null; cells.push(best); current = best; } return cells; } // 把一串格子拉直成路径点，加进 points。不含第一个格子（那是当前位置）。 // final 是最后一个格子里的确切位置（目标点或梯道口），给了就用它代替最后一个格子的中心。 function pushCells(points, layer, cells, final) { let index = 0, last = cells.length - 1, added = 0, margin = layer.margin, worst = []; while (index < last) { // worst[k]：原路从 index 到 index + k 这一段里最贴墙的格子有多贴墙。抄近路不能比它更贴墙。 let next = index + 1, far = Math.min(last, index + settings.smoothWindow); worst.length = 0; for (let k = index, most = 0; k <= far; k++) worst.push((most = Math.max(most, margin[cells[k]]))); for (let probe = far; probe > index + 1; probe--) if (lineClear(layer, cells[index], cells[probe], worst[probe - index])) { next = probe; break; } points.push(new THREE.Vector3(cellX(cells[next]), layer.y, cellZ(cells[next]))); added++; index = next; } if (final) { if (added) points[points.length - 1].copy(final); else points.push(final.clone()); } } // 梯道上的路径点：从 fromS 往 dir 方向，之后的每个折点 function pushLink(points, link, dir, fromS) { let add = (i) => { let point = new THREE.Vector3(link.points[i][0], link.heightAt(link.at[i]), link.points[i][1]); point.link = link; point.dir = dir; point.s = link.at[i]; points.push(point); }; if (dir > 0) { for (let i = 0; i < link.at.length; i++) if (link.at[i] > fromS + 1e-3) add(i); } else for (let i = link.at.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (link.at[i] < fromS - 1e-3) add(i); } let endpoint = (e) => (e & 1 ? links[e >> 1].b : links[e >> 1].a); // 在梯道口组成的小图上找最近的走法。返回 { cost, sequence, byLink }： // sequence 是依次经过的梯道口，byLink[e] 为真表示是沿梯道走到 e 的，否则是在同一层走过去的。 function planLinks(start, goal) { let n = links.length * 2, distance = new Array(n).fill(Infinity), previous = new Array(n).fill(-1), byLink = new Array(n).fill(false), done = new Array(n).fill(false), best = Infinity, bestEnd = -1; for (let e = 0; e < n; e++) { let end = endpoint(e); if (end.link.ok && end.layer === start.layer) distance[e] = end.field[start.cell] * cell; } for (;;) { let u = -1; for (let e = 0; e < n; e++) if (!done[e] && distance[e] < Infinity && (u < 0 || distance[e] < distance[u])) u = e; if (u < 0 || distance[u] >= best) break; done[u] = true; let end = endpoint(u); if (end.layer === goal.layer) { let total = distance[u] + end.field[goal.cell] * cell; if (total < best) { best = total; bestEnd = u; } } let across = u ^ 1, viaLink = distance[u] + end.link.cost; if (viaLink < distance[across]) { distance[across] = viaLink; previous[across] = u; byLink[across] = true; } for (let e = 0; e < n; e++) { if (e === u || e === across) continue; let other = endpoint(e); if (!other.link.ok || other.layer !== end.layer) continue; let walk = distance[u] + other.field[end.cell] * cell; if (walk < distance[e]) { distance[e] = walk; previous[e] = u; byLink[e] = false; } } } if (bestEnd < 0) return null; let sequence = []; for (let e = bestEnd; e >= 0; e = previous[e]) sequence.push(e); sequence.reverse(); return { cost: best, sequence, byLink }; } // 从 start（层和格子）走到 goal，路径点加进 points。走不到返回 false。 function route(points, start, goal, goalPoint) { let sameLayer = start.layer === goal.layer; if (sameLayer && start.cell === goal.cell) { points.push(goalPoint); return true; } let via = planLinks(start, goal), direct = null; if (sameLayer) { let di = Math.abs((start.cell % width) - (goal.cell % width)), dj = Math.abs(Math.floor(start.cell / width) - Math.floor(goal.cell / width)), straight = (di + dj - 0.5858 * Math.min(di, dj)) * cell; // 绕梯道的走法不可能比直线距离短；只有它确实更短时才不用搜 if (!via || via.cost > straight) direct = searchLayer(start.layer, start.cell, goal.cell); } if (direct && (!via || direct.cost <= via.cost)) { pushCells(points, start.layer, direct.cells, goalPoint); return true; } if (!via) return false; let at = start; for (let index = 0; index < via.sequence.length; index++) { let e = via.sequence[index], end = endpoint(e); if (index > 0 && via.byLink[e]) { let dir = e & 1 ? 1 : -1; pushLink(points, end.link, dir, dir > 0 ? 0 : end.link.length); } else { let cells = descend(end.field, end.layer, at.cell); if (!cells) return false; pushCells(points, end.layer, cells, end.point); } at = end; } let cells = descend(at.field, at.layer, goal.cell); if (!cells) return false; cells.reverse(); pushCells(points, goal.layer, cells, goalPoint); return true; } nav = { cell, width, height, layers, links, problems, settings, version: 0, buildMs: 0, lastExpanded: 0, rebuild: build, locate, layerNear, layerByName, // y 不给时查第一层（地面） isWalkable(x, z, y) { let layer = y === undefined ? layers[0] : layerNear(y); if (!layer) return false; let c = cellAt(x, z); return c >= 0 && !layer.blocked[c]; }, // agent 可以不给。给了并且它正在梯道上（agent.navRail），路径从
开始。 findPath(from, to, agent) { let goal = locate(to); if (!goal) return null; let points = [], rail = agent && agent.navRail && agent.navRail.link.ok ? agent.navRail : null, start; points.layered = true; if (rail) { let exit = rail.dir > 0 ? rail.link.b : rail.link.a; pushLink(points, rail.link, rail.dir, rail.s); start = { layer: exit.layer, cell: exit.cell }; } else start = locate(from); if (!start) return null; let goalPoint = goal.exact ? new THREE.Vector3(to.x, goal.layer.y, to.z) : new THREE.Vector3(cellX(goal.cell), goal.layer.y, cellZ(goal.cell)); if (!route(points, start, goal, goalPoint)) return rail ? points : null; // 路径是从起点所在格子的中心算的。站在格子边上时，到第一个路径点的直线可能擦过障碍格：先回格子中心。 if (!rail && points.length && !points[0].link && !nav.segmentClear(from, points[0])) points.unshift(new THREE.Vector3(cellX(start.cell), start.layer.y, cellZ(start.cell))); return points; }, randomPoint(center, radius) { let home = locate(center), layer = home ? home.layer : layers[0]; for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 24; attempt++) { let angle = Math.random() * 6.2832, distance = Math.sqrt(Math.random()) * radius, x = center.x + Math.cos(angle) * distance, z = center.z + Math.sin(angle) * distance, c = cellAt(x, z); if (c >= 0 && !layer.blocked[c]) return new THREE.Vector3(x, layer.y, z); } return home ? new THREE.Vector3(cellX(home.cell), layer.y, cellZ(home.cell)) : new THREE.Vector3(center.x, layer.y, center.z); }, clearLine(from, to) { let layer = layerNear(from.y || 0); if (!layer) return false; let a = cellAt(from.x, from.z), b = cellAt(to.x, to.z); return a >= 0 && b >= 0 && lineClear(layer, a, b, 2); }, // 从 from 到 to 的直线是不是全程都在可走的格子里。和 clearLine 不同，这里按确切的坐标算，不是按格子中心算： // 站在格子边上时，到目标的直线可能擦过旁边的障碍格，而从格子中心出发的直线不会。 // limit 是途中允许的最高 margin（不给就是 2，只要可走就行）。 segmentClear(from, to, limit = 2) { let layer = layerNear(from.y || 0); if (!layer) return false; let dx = to.x - from.x, dz = to.z - from.z, steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx, dz) / (cell * 0.25))); for (let k = 0; k <= steps; k++) { let c = cellAt(from.x + (dx * k) / steps, from.z + (dz * k) / steps); if (c < 0 || layer.blocked[c] || layer.margin[c] > limit) return false; } return true; }, // (x, z) 是不是在障碍里面或紧贴着障碍（机头半径以内）。y 用来选层。 solidAt(x, z, y) { let layer = layerNear(y || 0); if (!layer) return false; let c = cellAt(x, z); return c >= 0 && !!layer.solid[c]; }, // 把一个位置挪到最近的可走格子。本来就在可走格子上时原样返回。找不到返回 null。 snap(position) { let found = locate(position); if (!found) return null; return found.exact ? { x: position.x, y: found.layer.y, z: position.z, layer: found.layer, moved: 0 } : { x: cellX(found.cell), y: found.layer.y, z: cellZ(found.cell), layer: found.layer, moved: Math.hypot(cellX(found.cell) - position.x, cellZ(found.cell) - position.z), }; }, // 调试：各层和各梯道的概况 describe() { return { cell, buildMs: nav.buildMs, layers: layers.map((layer) => ({ name: layer.name, y: layer.y, free: layer.free, pruned: layer.pruned })), links: links.map((link) => ({ name: link.name, ok: link.ok, from: link.from && link.from.name, to: link.to && link.to.name, length: +link.length.toFixed(2), rise: +(link.rise || 0).toFixed(2), })), problems: problems.slice(), }; }, // 调试：把一层的一块区域画成字符。# 障碍，. 可走，x 走不到。每行是一个 z，从小到大。 dump(layerName, x0, z0, x1, z1) { let layer = layerByName(layerName), rows = []; for (let z = z0 + cell / 2; z < z1; z += cell) { let row =
+ (z - cell / 2).toFixed(2)); } return rows; }, }; build(); return nav; } // 物理模块 build() 时调用。地图登记了 nav，就按它建多层寻路并挂到 physics.nav.layered 上。 function installLayeredNav(G, physics) { let map = G.map, level = G.level; if (!map || typeof map.nav !==
|| !level || level.stub) return null; let definition = map.nav(G, level); if (!definition || !definition.layers || !definition.layers.length) return null; let nav = createLayeredNav(G, physics, definition); physics.nav.layered = nav; // 地图可以在多层寻路建好之后再做一些事（例如加楼上的出生点），这时已经能查格子了 if (definition.ready) definition.ready(nav); return nav; } // 霜脊站的多层寻路登记：三层、五条梯道，以及二层和屋顶的敌人出生点、掩体点。 // 登记的格式见 44-nav-layers.js 开头的说明。这里的坐标都是照着 40-frost-level.js 里的灰盒写的， // 那边的楼梯或墙挪了位置，这里要跟着改；改错了不会悄悄出问题：梯道建的时候会沿线探一遍碰撞盒， // 对不上的记在 G.physics.nav.layered.problems 里，__navTest() 也会报出来。 // // 三层： // ground 0 室外地面和主站楼一层 // f2 3.4 主站楼二层、南侧外廊和钢梯中间平台、连廊、舱段屋顶平台（都在同一个标高，连成一片） // roof 6.8 主站楼屋顶 // 连廊和它两头的楼板同高，所以不是梯道，就是 f2 层的一部分。架空舱段里面（1.2 米）没有登记，机器人不进去。 // // 梯道的折线画在梯道的中线上。机器人身长约 1.25 米（头在前 0.76，尾在后 0.49），梯道净宽只有 1.04 到 1.3 米， // 所以拐弯处都倒成圆弧，圆弧的半径是按
算的，不能随手改小。 var FROST_NAV_STAIRS = { // 室内楼梯间（东北角）。一层到二层、二层到屋顶两段在平面上完全重合： // 从楼梯间门口 (21, -12.3) 出发，沿南侧一跑（中线 z = -11.6）向东上到休息平台， // 在平台上掉头，沿北侧一跑（中线 z = -12.95）向西上到上一层，回到 (21, -12.3) 的正上方。 indoor: navRoundCorners( [ [21, -12.3], [21.8, -11.75], [22.6, -11.6], [25.055, -11.6], [25.055, -12.95], [22.6, -12.95], [21.8, -12.8], [21, -12.3], ], 0.675, ), // 南侧室外钢梯的下段：地面 (9, 14.1) → 向东上 16 级 → 中间平台 (15.6, 13.8) steelLower: navRoundCorners( [ [9, 14.1], [14.7, 14.1], [15.6, 13.8], ], 0.95, ), // 上段：中间平台 (18, 13.8) → 向东上 16 级 → 顶部平台上左转 → 穿过女儿墙的缺口到屋顶 (24.1, 10.9) steelUpper: navRoundCorners( [ [18, 13.8], [18.8, 14.1], [24.1, 14.1], [24.1, 10.9], ], 0.95, ), // 舱段屋顶平台西侧的楼梯：地面 (-14.6, 6.4) → 向南上 16 级 → 小平台上左转 → 平台 (-12.8, 13) podPlatform: navRoundCorners( [ [-14.6, 6.4], [-14.6, 13.1], [-12.8, 13], ], 1, ), }; gameMaps.frost.nav = function (G, level) { let { F2, ROOF } = FROST, THREE = G.THREE; return { layers: [ { name:
