); source = source.split(from).join(to); }; // 1. 地平线颜色换成上面那份 swap(Ng, frostSkyHorizonChunk,
); // 2. 雾的高度分布：贴着地面的一层最浓（吹雪和薄雾，让远处的地面和建筑脚下发虚）， // 往上几米迅速变薄，高处只留很淡的一层，所以山顶比山脚清楚 swap(
雾的高度分布
远处雾的颜色
, { fragmentShader: frostSkyFragmentShader, patchVolumetric: frostSkyPatchVolumetric, }); // 霜脊站的天气：被风横着吹的雪粒、贴着地面流动的雪流、峰顶的雪烟。由远景模块（41-frost-backdrop.js）创建和更新。 // // 雪粒 实例化的小面片，在相机周围循环。分三层：近层（14 米见方，跟着相机的高度）、 // 中层（36 米）、远层（84 米）；中层和远层越贴近地面越密。每一粒沿运动方向拉成一道短线。 // 贴地雪流 三张叠在一起的水平面片（离地 7、20、40 厘米），跟着相机走。图案按世界坐标算， // 顺风拉长并随风滚动；视线越贴近地面，穿过的雪越厚，所以远处的地面和建筑脚下发虚。 // 最低的一层另外画雪流投在雪面上的淡影。 // 雪烟 主脊几座高峰的峰顶被风刮起、向下风方向拖出去的雪带。每条是一张竖着的、顺风的面片。 // // 雪粒和贴地雪流都读太阳的阴影贴图：飘进建筑影子里的雪只剩天光的颜色，飘回阳光里才发亮。 // 室内范围（level.interiorBoxes）里没有雪。薄雾不在这里，是体积雾的参数（43-frost-map.js 的 post）。 var frostWeatherShared =
uniform float uTime, uTravel, uPix, uStreak, uAmount; uniform vec3 uWind, uSunDir, uSunCol, uAmb; attribute vec4 aSeed; varying vec3 vCol; varying float vA; varying vec2 vUv; ${frostWeatherShared} float frostPhase(float c){ float g = 0.72; return (1.0 - g * g) / (12.566 * pow(1.0 + g * g - 2.0 * g * c, 1.5)); } void main(){ vUv = position.xy; float layer = aSeed.w < 0.20 ? 0.0 : (aSeed.w < 0.58 ? 1.0 : 2.0); vec3 box = layer < 0.5 ? vec3(14.0, 7.0, 14.0) : (layer < 1.5 ? vec3(36.0, 11.0, 36.0) : vec3(84.0, 15.0, 84.0)); float r1 = fract(aSeed.w * 17.31), r2 = fract(aSeed.w * 5.77 + aSeed.x * 3.1), r3 = fract(aSeed.w * 41.7 + aSeed.z); float speed = 0.72 + 0.56 * r1; // 顺风走，外加阵风里的左右摆动 vec3 p = aSeed.xyz * box + uWind * (uTravel * speed); float swirlPhase = aSeed.x * 31.0 + aSeed.z * 17.0; p.xz += vec2(sin(uTime * 1.7 + swirlPhase), cos(uTime * 1.3 + swirlPhase * 0.7)) * (0.16 + 0.10 * layer); p.xz = mod(p.xz - cameraPosition.xz + 0.5 * box.xz, box.xz) - 0.5 * box.xz + cameraPosition.xz; // 缓慢下落，带一点起伏 float bob = sin(uTime * 2.3 + swirlPhase * 1.3); float yy = fract(aSeed.y - uTime * (0.03 + 0.04 * r2) + bob * 0.012); p.y = layer < 0.5 ? cameraPosition.y + (yy - 0.5) * box.y : yy * yy * box.y + 0.03; bool gone = p.y < 0.03 || frostIndoors(p); // 沿运动方向拉成短线；离得远时宽度不小于一个像素，同时按比例调淡 vec3 dir = normalize(uWind + vec3(0.0, bob * 0.10 - 0.035, 0.0)); vec3 toP = p - cameraPosition; float dist = length(toP); vec3 side = normalize(cross(dir, toP)); float w0 = 0.008 + 0.011 * r3; float w = max(w0, dist * uPix * 0.85); float len = max(uStreak * speed * (layer < 0.5 ? 0.55 : 1.0), w); vec3 wp = p + dir * (len * (position.y - 0.5)) + side * (w * position.x); vec2 e = abs(p.xz - cameraPosition.xz) / box.xz; float fade = smoothstep(0.7, 2.4, dist) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.36, 0.5, max(e.x, e.y))); // 贴着镜头的不画，免得像划痕 float c = dot(toP / max(dist, 1e-3), uSunDir); vCol = uAmb + uSunCol * frostSunlit(p) * (1.0 + 2.2 * frostPhase(c)); // 朝太阳看时雪粒透亮 vA = (w0 / w) * fade * (0.5 + 0.5 * r1) * uAmount; gl_Position = gone ? vec4(2.0, 2.0, 2.0, 1.0) : projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * vec4(wp, 1.0); }
uniform sampler2D tNoise, tDepth; uniform float uTime, uTravel, uAmount, uSoft; uniform vec2 uRes, uNearFar; uniform vec3 uWind, uSunDir, uSunCol, uAmb; varying vec3 vWorld; varying float vLayer; varying float vViewZ; ${frostWeatherShared} float n2(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f); return texture2D(tNoise, (i + f + 0.5) / 256.0).r; } // 不旋转的分形噪声：各层走向一致，拉长以后是一条条的 float sfbm(vec2 p){ float a = 0.5, s = 0.0; for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++){ s += a * n2(p); p = p * 2.07 + vec2(19.1, 7.7); a *= 0.5; } return s / 0.9375; } // (xz) 处第 layer 层雪流的浓度，0 到 1 多一点 float driftDensity(vec2 xz, float layer){ vec2 w = normalize(uWind.xz), wp = vec2(-w.y, w.x); float u = dot(xz, w) - uTravel * (0.50 + 0.14 * layer); // 贴地的一层比上面的慢 float v = dot(xz, wp) + layer * 7.3; // 阵风：一片一片地扫过去，两片之间的地面是干净的 float gust = smoothstep(0.36, 0.56, sfbm(vec2(u * 0.016, v * 0.028) + layer * 3.1)); // 雪流：顺风拉长，左右蜿蜒，不是笔直的平行线 float meander = (n2(vec2(u * 0.011, v * 0.020) + 9.0) - 0.5) * 14.0 + (n2(vec2(u * 0.045, v * 0.10) + 5.0) - 0.5) * 5.0; float snakes = sfbm(vec2(u * 0.10, (v + meander) * 0.55)); float fine = n2(vec2(u * 0.5, v * 3.1) + layer * 11.0); return smoothstep(0.45, 0.75, snakes) * gust * (0.55 + 0.75 * fine); } void main(){ if (frostIndoors(vWorld)) discard; vec3 toCam = cameraPosition - vWorld; float dist = length(toCam); vec3 vdir = toCam / max(dist, 1e-3); float dens = driftDensity(vWorld.xz, vLayer); // 一层雪流的厚度按视线和地面的夹角折算：越贴着地面看越浓 float slab = 0.10 / max(abs(vdir.y), 0.16); float a = (1.0 - exp(-dens * slab)) * uAmount; float lit = frostSunlit(vWorld); // 最贴地的一层另外画上面那层雪流投在雪面上的影子：太阳很低，影子落在背向太阳一米开外。 // 顺光时白色的雪流叠在白色的雪面上几乎看不出来，靠的就是这些流动的淡影。 float shade = 0.0; if (vLayer < 0.5) { shade = driftDensity(vWorld.xz + normalize(uSunDir.xz) * 1.1, 1.0); shade = smoothstep(0.15, 0.7, shade) * 0.42 * lit * uAmount * (1.0 - smoothstep(18.0, 46.0, dist)); } float fade = smoothstep(0.3, 1.2, dist) * (1.0 - smoothstep(22.0, 62.0, dist)); if (uSoft > 0.5) { // 和地面、物体相交的地方淡出，不留硬边 float d = texture2D(tDepth, gl_FragCoord.xy / uRes).x; float zs = (uNearFar.x * uNearFar.y) / ((uNearFar.y - uNearFar.x) * d - uNearFar.y); fade *= clamp((vViewZ - zs) / 0.30, 0.0, 1.0); } a *= fade; shade *= smoothstep(0.3, 1.2, dist); float total = a + shade * (1.0 - a); if (total < 0.004) discard; float forward = pow(max(dot(-vdir, uSunDir), 0.0), 5.0); // 逆光时发亮 // 雪流浓的地方是亮的；边缘稀薄处主要是天光的颜色。 // 低太阳下，悬在空中的雪比平躺着的雪面亮得多（雪面只接到两成的直射光） vec3 core = uAmb * 0.85 + uSunCol * lit * (1.0 + 1.2 * forward); vec3 fringe = uAmb * 0.85 + uSunCol * lit * (0.07 + 0.3 * forward); vec3 col = mix(fringe, core, smoothstep(0.12, 0.6, dens)); vec3 shadeCol = uAmb * 0.62; // 影子里的雪面只剩天光 gl_FragColor = vec4((col * a + shadeCol * shade * (1.0 - a)) / total, total); }
uniform sampler2D tNoise; uniform float uTime; uniform vec3 uColor; varying vec2 vUv; varying float vSeed; float n2(vec2 p){ vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p); f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f); return texture2D(tNoise, (i + f + 0.5) / 256.0).r; } float sfbm(vec2 p){ float a = 0.5, s = 0.0; for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++){ s += a * n2(p); p = p * 2.07 + vec2(19.1, 7.7); a *= 0.5; } return s / 0.9375; } void main(){ float t = vUv.x; // 0 在峰顶，1 在下风的尽头 // 轴线：离开峰顶后略微上扬，并被乱流推得上下起伏 float centre = 0.40 + 0.12 * t + (n2(vec2(t * 3.0 - uTime * 0.07, vSeed * 40.0)) - 0.5) * 0.34 * t; float width = mix(0.07, 0.42, pow(t, 0.75)); // 越往下风越散开 float y = (vUv.y - centre) / width; float body = exp(-y * y * 1.8); float n = sfbm(vec2(t * 5.0 - uTime * 0.10 + vSeed * 17.0, vUv.y * 4.0 + vSeed * 5.0 + t * 1.5)); float wisps = smoothstep(0.34, 0.72, n + (body - 0.5) * 0.4); float strength = 0.30 + 0.45 * fract(vSeed * 7.31); // 每座峰的雪烟强弱不同 float a = body * wisps * smoothstep(0.0, 0.06, t) * pow(1.0 - t, 1.7) * strength; if (a < 0.004) discard; gl_FragColor = vec4(uColor, a); }
, uniforms: { tNoise: { value: G.render.sky.material.uniforms.tNoise.value }, uTime: time, // 被低太阳照亮的雪雾 uColor: { value: new THREE.Vector3( en.horizonAway.r * 0.4 + Mn.color.r * Mn.intensity * 0.105, en.horizonAway.g * 0.4 + Mn.color.g * Mn.intensity * 0.105, en.horizonAway.b * 0.4 + Mn.color.b * Mn.intensity * 0.105, ), }, }, vertexShader:
; mesh.frustumCulled = false; mesh.renderOrder = 870; mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; mesh.castShadow = mesh.receiveShadow = false; return { mesh, triangles: anchors.length * 2 }; } function createFrostWeather(G, wind) { let THREE = G.THREE, root = new THREE.Group(), random = G.rng && G.rng.fork ? G.rng.fork() : Math.random, boxMin = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(() => new THREE.Vector3()), boxMax = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(() => new THREE.Vector3()), shared = { uTime: { value: 0 }, uTravel: { value: 0 }, // 风吹过的累计距离（米）。阵风让风速变化，所以不能直接用时间乘速度 uAmount: { value: 1 }, uWind: { value: new THREE.Vector3(wind.x, 0, wind.z) }, uSunDir: { value: Mn.dir.clone() }, // 雪粒被太阳照亮时的亮度，和只有天光时的颜色 uSunCol: { value: new THREE.Vector3(Mn.color.r, Mn.color.g, Mn.color.b).multiplyScalar(Mn.intensity * 0.14) }, uAmb: { value: new THREE.Vector3( en.zenith.r * 0.5 + en.horizonAway.r * 0.35, en.zenith.g * 0.5 + en.horizonAway.g * 0.35, en.zenith.b * 0.5 + en.horizonAway.b * 0.35, ), }, uBoxMin: { value: boxMin }, uBoxMax: { value: boxMax }, tShadow: { value: null }, uShadowMatrix: { value: new THREE.Matrix4() }, uHasShadow: { value: 0 }, }; root.name =
; // ---- 雪粒 ---- let count = Math.round(11000 * Math.max(0.3, G.quality.rain || 0.5)), snowGeometry = new THREE.InstancedBufferGeometry(), seeds = new Float32Array(count * 4); for (let i = 0; i < seeds.length; i++) seeds[i] = random(); snowGeometry.setAttribute(
; snow.frustumCulled = false; snow.renderOrder = 900; snow.matrixAutoUpdate = false; snow.castShadow = snow.receiveShadow = false; // ---- 贴地雪流 ---- let heights = [0.07, 0.2, 0.4], half = 85, positions = [], layers = [], indices = []; heights.forEach((y, layer) => { let base = positions.length / 3; positions.push(-half, y, -half, half, y, -half, -half, y, half, half, y, half); layers.push(layer, layer, layer, layer); indices.push(base, base + 2, base + 1, base + 1, base + 2, base + 3); }); let driftGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); driftGeometry.setAttribute(
; drift.frustumCulled = false; drift.renderOrder = 880; drift.castShadow = drift.receiveShadow = false; // 软相交要用场景深度。和特效模块一样，只在主相机渲染时读渲染模块的深度副本（上一帧的） drift.onBeforeRender = (renderer, scene, camera) => { let render = G.render, depth = render && render.depthTexture; if (depth && depth.image && camera === G.camera && render.depthTextureIsLive !== true && G.params.fxsoft !==
) { driftUniforms.tDepth.value = depth; driftUniforms.uSoft.value = 1; driftUniforms.uRes.value.set(render.resolution.x, render.resolution.y); driftUniforms.uNearFar.value.set(camera.near, camera.far); } else { driftUniforms.tDepth.value = null; driftUniforms.uSoft.value = 0; } }; root.add(drift, snow); let clock = 0, travel = 0, size = new THREE.Vector2(); return { root, snow, drift, wind, uniforms: shared, triangles: count * 2 + heights.length * 2, drawCalls: 2, // 雪的多少，0 到 1 以上：同时调雪粒和贴地雪流的浓度 setAmount(amount) { shared.uAmount.value = Math.max(0, amount); }, update(dt) { clock += dt; // 阵风：风速在平均值的五成到一倍半之间慢慢起伏 let gust = 1 + 0.32 * Math.sin(clock * 0.31) + 0.18 * Math.sin(clock * 0.83 + 1.3); travel += dt * wind.speed * gust; shared.uTime.value = clock; shared.uTravel.value = travel; let camera = G.camera; if (camera) { let resolution = G.render && G.render.resolution; if (resolution && resolution.x > 8) size.copy(resolution); else G.renderer.getDrawingBufferSize(size); snowUniforms.uPix.value = (2 * Math.tan((camera.fov * Math.PI) / 360)) / Math.max(1, size.y); drift.position.set(camera.position.x, 0, camera.position.z); } let interior = (G.level && G.level.interiorBoxes) || []; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { let room = interior[i]; if (room && room.min && room.max) { boxMin[i].set(room.min.x - 0.15, room.min.y - 1, room.min.z - 0.15); boxMax[i].set(room.max.x + 0.15, room.max.y + 0.3, room.max.z + 0.15); } else { boxMin[i].set(0, 0, 0); boxMax[i].set(0, 0, 0); } } let sun = G.render && G.render.sun, map = sun && sun.shadow && sun.shadow.map; shared.tShadow.value = map ? map.texture : null; shared.uHasShadow.value = map ? 1 : 0; if (map) shared.uShadowMatrix.value.copy(sun.shadow.matrix); }, }; } // 霜脊站的环境声：冰原上的大风。在 43-frost-map.js 的 ambience 里登记，由音频模块（10-audio.js） // 和其他声音一起在后台合成，顶替港口图那份较轻的风声。 // // 一段 12 秒的循环，四层叠在一起，都跟着同一条阵风曲线起伏： // 低沉的轰隆（150 赫兹以下的噪声）——风压在身上、压在建筑上的声音 // 呼啸的主体（中心频率随阵风在 400 到 700 赫兹之间滑动的噪声） // 哨音（很窄的带通，1000 赫兹上下滑动）——风穿过桅杆、拉线和栏杆，只在阵风最强时出现 // 沙沙声（3800 赫兹以上的噪声）——雪粒打在外壳上 // 阵风曲线的周期都能整除 12 秒，所以首尾接得上；末尾多算半秒用来和开头交叉淡化。 function* synthFrostWind(sampleRate, random) { let seconds = 12, total = seconds + 0.5, rumble = Qn(sampleRate, total, random), roar = Qn(sampleRate, total, random), whistle = Qn(sampleRate, total, random), hiss = Qn(sampleRate, total, random), turn = Math.PI * 2, // 参数是 0 到 1 的进度（滤波函数这样调用），换成秒再算 gustAt = (time) => 0.5 + 0.32 * Math.sin((turn * time) / 12 + 0.4) + 0.18 * Math.sin((turn * time) / 4 + 1.9), howlAt = (time) => 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin((turn * time) / 6 + 2.2); yield; tn(rumble,
, 3800, 0.7, sampleRate); yield; for (let i = 0; i < rumble.length; i++) { let time = i / sampleRate, gust = gustAt(time), howl = howlAt(time) * gust, flutter = 0.75 + 0.25 * Math.sin(turn * time * 7.3 + 3 * Math.sin(turn * time * 0.9)); rumble[i] = rumble[i] * 3 * (0.55 + 0.45 * gust) + roar[i] * (0.25 + 0.75 * gust * gust) + whistle[i] * 1.6 * howl * howl * howl + hiss[i] * 0.1 * (0.15 + 0.85 * gust * gust) * flutter; } return Rv(rumble, Math.round(sampleRate * seconds), true); } // 自动走查和整局流程测试。调试工具，只挂在 window 上，不参与正式游戏逻辑。 // // window.__walkTest() 沿关卡登记的路线，用物理模块驱动一个虚拟胶囊走一遍，报告每条路线是否走通 // window.__missionTest() 依次切断三处中继再到撤离点，报告最终的游戏状态（返回 Promise） // // 运行方式： // node tools/verify.mjs --query
// // 路线登记在关卡模块返回对象的 walkRoutes 字段里，格式： // { name:
